Existing safety benchmarks, however, remain largely prompt-centric or tied to fixed conditioning interfaces, leaving such compositional risks difficult to study systematically. To bridge this gap, we introduce Multi2AV-Safety, the first safety benchmark, to the best of our knowledge, to cover all 11 non-singleton T/I/A/V conditioning configurations for audio-video generation, comprising 11,024 attack instances. Evaluation on Multi2AV-Safety reveals systematic weaknesses in representative multimodal safety guards across attack mechanisms and harm-evidence structures. Our evaluation reveals two complementary failure modes: harmful semantics can emerge from the combination of individually benign inputs, while explicit harmful cues can become harder to detect when mixed with benign multimodal context. Together, these results identify \emph{compositional risk perception} as a central capability gap in safeguarding multimodal-conditioned audio-video generation: current safety guards fail to reliably integrate safety evidence across modalities and time, even when all conditioning inputs are observable. The dataset will be publicly released in October 2026.

</details>


### [257] [DuMateBench: Evaluating Autonomous Agents in Complex Real-World Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26546)
*Zechun Niu,Yukun Zhao,Jiaxin Zhang,Xu Shen,Jinhua Si,Han Tian,Can Xu,Yunfan Song,Jiaxin Mao,Yansong Gao,Yuchen Li,Jianmin Wu,Lingyong Yan,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DuMateBench 是一个从大规模生产级智能体平台中提取的真实用户会话重构的基准测试，包含 200 个任务、8 大场景和 17 个细粒度能力类别，在 Docker 隔离容器中注入三种现实环境复杂性（资源不足、不稳定、噪声），并通过混合“确定性 + LLM 评判”协议评估性能。实验表明，跨五个智能体框架与四个先进 LLM 的严格任务完成率存在显著差距，鲁棒性受 LLM 与框架能力共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常按应用或能力隔离任务，且评测环境比实际部署更干净、更稳定，无法反映真实世界中的多工具复杂工作流和环境扰动，因此缺乏对真实场景下自主智能体能力的可信评估。

Method: 从生产级智能体平台收集经过匿名化与隐私审查的真实用户会话，保留解前交互历史、持久配置与工作区状态，经人工验证构建 200 个任务；将任务部署在隔离 Docker 容器中，注入 Insufficient（资源不足）、Unstable（不稳定）、Noisy（噪声）三种环境复杂性；采用确定性检查与 LLM-as-Judge 结合的混合评估协议，并结合鲁棒性、效率与诊断分析。

Result: 在五个自主智能体框架联合四个 SOTA LLM 的实验中发现严格任务完成率存在显著差距；性能受环境扰动影响，且由 LLM 能力与外围智能体框架共同塑造；同时提供了补充的鲁棒性、效率与诊断分析结果。

Conclusion: 真实构建的 DuMateBench 揭示出现有智能体系统在现实环境复杂性下完成任务的能力仍有实质不足，评估结果表明提升智能体鲁棒性需要同时改进底层 LLM 与智能体框架的协调能力；代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly adopted to complete complex, multi-tool workflows in real-world settings. However, existing benchmarks typically separate tasks by application or capability and evaluate agents in environments that are cleaner and more stable than those encountered in practice. We introduce DuMateBench, a real-session benchmark reconstructed from anonymized and privacy-screened user sessions collected from a large-scale production agent platform. Each task preserves the relevant pre-solution interaction history, persistent configurations, and workspace state, and is then validated through human verification. The resulting benchmark comprises 200 tasks spanning 8 broad scenarios and 17 fine-grained capability categories, with most tasks requiring multiple capability coordination. We execute these tasks in isolated Docker containers injected with three forms of real-world environmental complexity: Insufficient, Unstable, and Noisy, and assess performance using a hybrid deterministic and LLM-as-Judge evaluation protocol. Experiments across five representative autonomous-agent frameworks paired with four state-of-the-art LLMs reveal substantial gaps in strict task completion. Complementary robustness, efficiency, and diagnostic analyses further show that performance under environmental perturbations is jointly shaped by the capabilities of the LLM and the surrounding agent framework. The code and data are publicly available at https://dumatebench.com/.

</details>


### [258] [AgentJudgeBench: A Multi-Difficulty Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Judges on Agentic Tool-Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26623)
*Abhigya Verma,Amit Kumar Saha,Seganrasan Subramanian,Sai Harshitha Aluru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentJudgeBench，第一个系统研究 LLM 作为评测器（LLM-as-a-judge）在基于 DAG 工作流的智能体工具调用场景中可靠性的基准。包含 3,808 个实例、六种 DAG 拓扑和三个难度层，使用五个生成器和六个评测器在有无真值条件下评估。研究发现评测器对齐度随任务难度单调下降，无真值时下降速度快 1.5 倍，且所有评测器在困难查询上收敛到 77-82% 的狭窄区间，揭示出模型容量无法克服的结构性天花板；真值暴露并非总有益（对部分前沿模型反而降低对齐度，属过度锚定）；思维链和温度几乎无效，结构化评分标准最多提升 6.5pp 但缺乏普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 评测器被广泛用于评估智能体工具调用系统，但它们在结构化、依赖驱动的工作流上的可靠性此前几乎未被研究。现有 LLM-as-a-judge 研究多集中于开放式文本或偏好评估，本文旨在填补这一空白，系统考察评测器在流程化 DAG 工作流评测中的真实能力与局限，并为可靠评测提供实用指导。

Method: 构建 AgentJudgeBench 基准，涵盖 3,808 个实例、六种 DAG 拓扑和三个难度层；使用五个生成器（3B-70B 开源模型及 GPT-5.4）与六个评测器（20B 到前沿规模），在提供/不提供真值（with-/without-ground-truth）两种配对条件下进行对照实验；并设计消融性缓解策略实验（思维链、评测温度、结构化评分标准）及人类验证研究。

Result: 评测器对齐度随任务难度单调下降，无真值时下降速度快 1.5 倍；在无真值的困难查询上，六个评测器无论规模大小均收敛于 77-82% 的窄带，表明存在主要由任务难度驱动的结构性天花板（其高度对较弱生成器部分取决于提示）。真值暴露并非普遍有益：分别使 GPT-5.4 对齐度下降 1.5pp、Gemini-2.5-Pro 下降 3.9pp（过度锚定）。缓解策略中思维链推理与温度设置影响可忽略，结构化评分标准最多提升 6.5pp 但无法在评测器-生成器组合间统一泛化。有真值时 QwQ-32B 与程序化参考最一致，人类验证研究确定 GPT-OSS-120B 与人类判断最对齐；无真值时前沿评测器仅在天花板范围内微弱领先。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 评测器在结构化智能体工作流评测中存在根本性局限，任务难度构成模型容量无法逾越的结构性上限，真值信息可能引发过度锚定，常见缓解手段（如思维链、调温）收效甚微。研究结果为智能体系统可靠评测提供了实用指南，指明需要重新审视评测器设计及其与生成器、任务结构的匹配关系。

Abstract: LLM judges are widely used to evaluate agentic tool-calling systems, yet their reliability on structured, dependency-driven workflows remains largely unexamined. We present AgentJudgeBench, the first benchmark to systematically study LLM-as-a-judge reliability for agentic tool-calling over workflow DAGs, as distinct from the broader LLM-as-a-judge task of open-ended text or preference evaluation. The benchmark comprises 3,808 instances spanning six DAG topologies and three difficulty tiers, evaluated with five generators (3B-70B open-weight models and GPT-5.4) and six judges (20B to frontier scale) under paired with- and without-ground-truth conditions. Judge alignment degrades monotonically with task difficulty, 1.5x faster without ground truth, and on hard queries without ground truth all six judges converge to a narrow 77-82% band regardless of scale, revealing a structural ceiling driven primarily by task difficulty, though its height is partly prompt-dependent for weaker generators, that model capacity alone cannot overcome. Ground-truth exposure is not uniformly beneficial: it reduces alignment for GPT-5.4 (1.5 pp) and Gemini-2.5-Pro (3.9 pp), consistent with over-anchoring. Among mitigation strategies, chain-of-thought reasoning and judge temperature both have negligible effect, while structured evaluation rubrics improve alignment by up to 6.5 pp but do not generalize uniformly across judge-generator pairs. With ground truth, QwQ-32B best matches the programmatic reference, while a human validation study identifies GPT-OSS-120B as the most human-aligned judge; without it, frontier judges lead only marginally within the shared ceiling. These results expose fundamental limitations of current LLM judges and yield practical guidelines for reliable evaluation in agentic systems.

</details>


### [259] [SIGMA: Structured Noise-Effect-Aware Grouped Multi-Agent Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26683)
*Li Mingqian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SIGMA，一种利用协作结构学习鲁棒表示的分层协作框架，用于在噪声观测下保持多智能体强化学习的协调能力，并在星际争霸II的噪声观测任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作式多智能体强化学习在噪声观测下难以保持鲁棒协调。尽管观测扰动常以独立方式施加于各智能体，但其对协作决策的下游影响会通过底层协作结构变得结构化。作者将此现象刻画为"结构化噪声效应"：噪声引起的决策偏差在任务依赖较强的相邻智能体间呈现局部相关性，同时在全局上跨智能体和局部结构呈现异质性。然而现有鲁棒MARL方法很少显式刻画或利用这种结构依赖的噪声效应。

Method: SIGMA采用分层协作框架：(1) 通过基于密度的分组将智能体组织为自适应局部结构；(2) 进行组内共识聚合，保留共享任务相关信息同时平滑智能体特有的表征偏差；(3) 通过组间注意力自适应地整合不同组的信息，在保持全局协调的同时容纳各组异质性的贡献。

Result: 在星际争霸II的噪声观测任务上，实验经验性地验证了结构化噪声效应的存在，并证明SIGMA在观测噪声下持续提升鲁棒性，同时在无噪声环境中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 结构化噪声效应是协同MARL中的关键现象，显式利用协作结构（分层分组+共识聚合+组间注意力）能有效提升噪声条件下的鲁棒性，且不会牺牲无噪声情况下的性能，为鲁棒MARL提供了新的结构化视角。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) faces significant challenges in maintaining robust coordination under noisy observations. Although observation disturbances are often introduced independently across agents, their downstream effects on cooperative decision-making can become structured through underlying cooperation structures. We characterize this phenomenon as structured noise effects, where noise-induced decision effects exhibit local correlation among agents with stronger task-related dependencies while remaining globally heterogeneous across different agents and local structures. Existing robust MARL methods, however, rarely explicitly characterize or exploit such structure-dependent noise effects. To address this limitation, we propose SIGMA, a hierarchical collaboration framework that exploits cooperation structures to learn robust representations under noisy observations. SIGMA first organizes agents into adaptive local structures through density-based grouping and performs intra-group consensus aggregation to preserve shared task-relevant information while smoothing agent-specific representation deviations. Inter-group attention then adaptively integrates information across different groups to preserve global coordination while accommodating their heterogeneous contributions. Experiments on noisy-observation tasks in StarCraft II empirically validate the structured noise effects and demonstrate that SIGMA consistently improves robustness under observation noise while maintaining competitive performance in noise-free environments.

</details>


### [260] [Relational Over-Regularization: Graph-Based AI-Generated Text Detection via Sentence Transition Deviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26694)
*Hyeonchu Park,Bugeun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对AI生成文本检测在分布偏移下失效的问题，识别出句对层面"关系性过度正则化"（ROR）这一结构信号——LLM的句间过渡方差因段落边界处的重复性相似爆发和模板化过渡而被抬高，偏离人类写作。作为该关系问题设定的一种具体实例化，提出跨源文体指纹图（CSFG），以可学习的GNN边特征编码位置、时序、语义与过渡偏差信号，在二分类检测中达到97.14%准确率、1.57%误报率，优于最强图基线11.14个百分点，并对未见LLM具有稳健泛化能力，但若生成器过渡方差不高于人类基线则检测性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGT检测方法依赖词元级统计信号或独立的文体特征，容易过拟合特定生成器，在分布偏移下失效；需要一种不依赖具体生成器、且能泛化的结构级信号。

Method: (1) 识别句对级结构信号：LLM的句间过渡方差偏离人类写作，表现为段落边界处重复性相似爆发与模板化过渡导致方差膨胀；(2) 将其形式化为关系性过度正则化（ROR），并在四个基准上验证（p<0.001）；(3) 提出跨源文体指纹图（CSFG），一种基于GNN的框架，将位置、时序、语义和过渡偏差信号编码为可学习的边特征；(4) 利用每条边的符号化偏差δ_ij免手工阈值地实例化ROR，并作为误报校准器。作者强调核心贡献是关系性问题设定本身，而非新的GNN架构。

Result: CSFG在二分类检测上达97.14%准确率，较最强图基线高出11.14个百分点，误报率仅1.57%；在方差膨胀区间内对未见LLM具有稳健泛化；但对过渡方差等于或低于人类基线的生成器，检测性能会退化。

Conclusion: 关系性过度正则化（ROR）提供了一种可泛化、可解释的AIGT检测视角；CSFG是该信号的一种有效运算化方案，但检测边界受限于生成器是否处于方差膨胀区间。

Abstract: Detecting AI-generated text (AIGT) remains challenging because existing approaches rely on token-level statistical signals or independent stylometric features, causing them to overfit to specific generators and fail under distribution shift. We identify a structural signal at the sentence-pair level: LLMs produce inter-sentence transition variance that deviates from human writing through inflated variance driven by recurring similarity bursts at paragraph boundaries and templated transitions. We formalize this as Relational Over-Regularization (ROR) and validate it across four benchmarks (p < 0.001). The central contribution is this relational problem formulation, not a novel GNN architecture; CSFG is one concrete instantiation for operationalizing ROR. To exploit this signal, we propose the Cross-Source Stylometric Fingerprint Graph (CSFG), a graph-based framework that encodes positional, sequential, semantic, and transition deviation signals as learnable GNN edge features. The per-edge signed deviation δ_ij operationalizes ROR without hand-crafted thresholds and acts as a false-positive calibrator. CSFG achieves 97.14% accuracy under binary detection, outperforming the strongest graph-based baseline by 11.14 pp, with a false-positive rate of 1.57% and robust generalization to unseen LLMs in the inflated-variance regime; detection degrades for generators whose transition variance falls at or below the human baseline.

</details>


### [261] [Five Primitives for Governing Autonomous AI Agents at Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26696)
*Jiten Oswal,John Cadeddu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对企业环境中自主AI代理的治理问题，论文提出治理本质上是运行时问题而非模型对齐或构建期问题，并推导出五个原语：发现（discovery）、身份（identity）、治理（governance）、证明（attestation）和供应链（supply chain）。实现中代理动作在执行前需经过策略调解、按租户动作词汇表授权，并记录在可第三方验证的哈希链签名账本中。作者明确说明四个原语已在私有试点中运行，第五个作为独立工具构建但尚未集成到请求路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署自主AI代理时沿用了为人类用户和长期服务设计的控制模型，但这种模型在三个方面不匹配：代理主体是短暂临时的，出现和消失速度快于供给（provisioning）；代理行为由模型选择而非编程决定，因此其可能尝试的动作集合无法预先知晓；代理群体是被发现而非被供给的，因为任何能调用API的人都可以创建代理。这种不匹配暴露出传统治理框架的根本缺陷。

Method: 论文论证治理自主代理是一个运行时问题（runtime problem），而非模型对齐问题或构建期问题。据此从"动作生效前和生效后必须回答的问题"推导出五个原语：发现、身份、治理、证明、供应链；对每个原语说明其缺失时会出现什么故障，以及为何其他原语在结构上无法替代它。实现方案中，代理的每个动作在生效前被策略调解（mediated against policy），按每租户动作词汇表（per-tenant action vocabulary）授权，并记录在哈希链签名账本（hash-linked signed ledger）中，该账本可在供应商不参与（out of the loop）的情况下由第三方验证。

Result: 论文报告了该架构的代价：执行点（enforcement point）位于请求的关键路径之上；身份需要每个工作负载一个边车（sidecar）；失败即关闭（fail-closed）的调解机制会把可用性事件转化为拒绝服务。实现状态透明公开：四个原语已构建并在私有试点中运行，第五个原语已作为独立工具构建但尚未集成到请求路径中。作者刻意将其保留在集合中，并指出"五个部分的分解恰好匹配作者恰好构建的东西"不构成分类法，而只是对代码库的描述——这体现了方法论上的自我批判。

Conclusion: 治理自主AI代理不能依赖为人类用户设计的既有控制模型，必须将其视为运行时问题，围绕动作生效前后必须回答的问题建立以发现、身份、治理、证明、供应链为原语的体系。该体系以关键路径上的执行点、身份边车和失败即关闭的调解为代价，换取动作执行前的策略调解、租户级词汇授权和可第三方验证的不可篡改审计记录。作者对实现状态保持诚实，不将自身构建范围夸大为普遍分类法。

Abstract: Enterprise deployments of autonomous AI agents inherit a control model built for human users and long-lived services, and the fit fails in three specific ways: agent principals are ephemeral, appearing and vanishing faster than provisioning; their actions are selected by a model rather than programmed, so the set of things they may attempt is not known in advance; and the population is discovered rather than provisioned, because anyone who can call an API can create one. We argue that governing such agents is a runtime problem -- not a model-alignment problem and not a build-time problem -- and we derive five primitives from the questions that must be answered before an action takes effect and after it has: discovery, identity, governance, attestation, and supply chain. For each we state what fails if it is absent and why the others cannot structurally supply it. We describe an implementation in which an agent's action is mediated against policy before it takes effect, authorised against a per-tenant action vocabulary, and recorded in a hash-linked signed ledger a third party can verify with the vendor out of the loop. We report what the architecture costs: the enforcement point sits on the request's critical path, identity requires a sidecar per workload, and fail-closed mediation converts availability incidents into denial. We are explicit about implementation status: four primitives are built and running in private pilots, and the fifth is built as separate tooling and not yet integrated into the request path. We keep it in the set deliberately: a five-part decomposition that exactly matches what its authors happened to build is not a taxonomy but a description of a codebase.

</details>


### [262] [Accelerating Scientific Research with Gemini in the Real-World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26701)
*Samuel Schmidgall,Xiaokai Zhu,Marian Shaw,Lin Yang,Valentin Liévin,Jingyun Yang,Yuchen Zhuang,Tim Strother,Alex Bijamov,Min Woo Sun,Anil Palepu,Justin Chen,David Steiner,Jacqueline Shreibati,Wei-Hung Weng,Yilin Zhao,Xingjian Hu,Nicholas Zahn,Sadhya Garg,Julia Kirby,Yuxiang Gan,Jiaoli Li,Divy Thakkar,Shekoofeh Azizi,David Racz,Juraj Gottweis,Vivek Natarajan,Chenglin Wu,Tal Danino,Keran Rong,Haozhe Wang,Benoit Schillings,Yong Cheng,Quoc V. Le,Tao Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Co-Scientist系统的扩展与全面真实世界验证。这是一个基于Gemini的多智能体系统，旨在加速从假设生成、实验执行到论文撰写的端到端科学研究。系统从在硅假设生成扩展到执行落地的研究伙伴，在材料科学（与半自动CVD反应器对接设计MXene前驱体路线、单次尝试生长单层MoS2/MoSe2/WS2）、生物学（预测工程大肠杆菌群体表型并与未发表湿实验数据定量吻合）、计算机科学（自主发现推理时扩展架构，在HealthBench上与六个前沿模型相比胜出）三大领域实现闭环验证。双盲研究（30名领域专家、450份评审）表明其可靠性模块能减少幻觉与抄袭、提升研究安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学AI多停留在in silico假设生成阶段，缺乏跨真实实验场景的端到端闭环验证。作者旨在将Co-Scientist从纯粹的假设生成器升级为执行落地的研究伙伴，填补多智能体科学AI在材料、生物、计算机等真实学科中端到端自动化的空白，并系统验证其可靠性。

Method: 基于Gemini的多智能体架构，采用针对执行落地的专门配置，支持闭环工作流（假设生成→实验设计→执行→分析→论文）。关键技术点包括：1) 与半自动CVD反应器接口，进行"lab-in-the-loop"快速执行；2) 借助Gemini 3 Deep Think在数分钟内根据实验室约束定制生长配方；3) 通过稀疏成像数据预测生物学表型；4) 内置可靠性模块以降低幻觉和抄袭；5) 采用双盲评估对端到端生成的论文进行专家评审。

Result: 材料学：设计的MXene前驱体路线实验产出层状二维材料，与Ti3C2Tx晶格结构相似（尚需进一步确认原子结构）；在数分钟内针对实验室约束定制配方，实现MoS2、MoSe2、WS2单层半导体的单次尝试生长。生物学：从稀疏成像数据定量预测了工程E. coli在IPTG梯度下的群体涌现表型，与未发表湿实验形态学测量吻合。计算机科学：自主发现推理时扩展架构，在HealthBench（Hard和Professional）上超越六个前沿模型，并在盲法医师评估中降低潜在临床伤害。评估：30位专家、450份评审的双盲研究表明可靠性模块降低幻觉与抄袭、改善研究安全。

Conclusion: 研究证明多智能体科学AI系统已能够闭环地加速真实世界科学发现，Co-Scientist在多个学科中展示了从实验设计到执行的端到端能力，且通过可靠性模块缓解了幻觉与抄袭风险。未来仍需更多实验确认部分材料结构，并进一步验证系统在更广泛科学任务中的泛化性与安全性。

Abstract: We present an extension and comprehensive real-world validation of Co-Scientist, a Gemini-based multi-agent system designed to accelerate end-to-end scientific research across hypothesis generation, experimentation, and manuscript generation. Moving beyond in silico hypothesis generation, this specialized configuration transitions Co-Scientist into an execution-grounded research partner advancing closed-loop scientific workflows across materials science, biology, and computer science. In materials science, Co-Scientist interfaced with a semi-automated chemical vapor deposition reactor to design a safe precursor route for MXenes; experimental execution produced a lamellar 2D material sharing key structural similarities with the Ti3C2Tx MXene lattice, although further experiments are needed to confirm the atomic structure. Leveraging Gemini 3 Deep Think for rapid, lab-in-the-loop execution, it also tailored growth recipes to laboratory constraints in minutes, enabling single-attempt growth of monolayer MoS2, MoSe2, and WS2 semiconductors. In biology, Co-Scientist predicted emergent swarming phenotypes of engineered E. coli across inducer (IPTG) gradients from sparse imaging data, quantitatively matching unpublished wet-lab morphological measurements. In computer science, Co-Scientist autonomously discovered an inference-time scaling architecture that outperformed six frontier models on HealthBench (Hard and Professional) while reducing potential clinical harm under blinded physician evaluation. Finally, a double-blind study of end-to-end generated papers with 30 domain experts across 450 reviews demonstrates that Co-Scientist's reliability modules reduce hallucination and plagiarism while improving research safety. Together, these results demonstrate progress toward closed-loop multi-agent scientific AI systems capable of accelerating real-world scientific discovery.

</details>


### [263] [Style as a Confound: False Positives in AI Detection of Non-Native Academic Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26710)
*Hyeonchu Park,Gahye Jeong,Bugeun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用专业编辑服务提供的135,389对原稿与编辑稿（2018-2025年），在控制内容与作者身份的前提下，考察13种AI文本检测器对母语非英语作者手稿及其经母语编辑修改后版本的反应。结果显示误报率从0.0%到100.0%不等，编辑对检测分数的影响方向随检测器而异，且影响程度与编辑量相关，证明专业编辑风格是AI检测器输出的关键混淆变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI文本检测器在学术环境中被广泛使用，但尚不清楚其输出反映的是真正的AI作者身份，还是与“润色后的学术英语”相关的语言学特征。以往研究报道非母语英语写作存在高误报率，但群体层面的比较将作者身份与主题、领域和写作风格混为一谈。专业编辑是理想的实验场景，因为它改变稿件的语言形式而保留作者身份与内容，从而可以厘清这一混淆。

Method: 收集某专业英语编辑服务在2018-2025年间的135,389对文档（非母语作者原稿与其母语编辑版本），在控制内容和作者身份的条件下，比较13种AI文本检测器对每对文档的判定差异，并分析与编辑程度的关系，同时量化人工写作文本的误报率。

Result: 13种检测器对人工写作文本的误报率差异极大，从0.0%到100.0%不等。同一编辑操作在不同检测器中产生相反方向的分数变化：有的提高AI分数，有的降低。分数变化与编辑量显著相关。

Conclusion: 研究识别出专业编辑风格是AI检测器输出中的关键混淆变量，而非实现了文本来源与语言风格的完全分离，这引发了对检测器在学术环境中公平性与可靠性的严重担忧。

Abstract: AI text detectors are increasingly employed in academic settings, but it remains unclear whether their outputs reflect AI authorship itself or broader linguistic features associated with polished academic English. Previous studies have reported high false-positive rates (FPRs) for non-native English writing, but population-level comparisons confound authorship with differences in topic, domain, and writing style. Professional editing provides a useful setting for examining this issue because it changes the linguistic form of manuscripts while preserving authorship and content. We examined 135,389 document pairs from a professional English editing service (2018-2025), comprising non-native manuscripts and their native-edited versions, to assess how editing affects detector responses controlling for content and authorship. For the 13 AI text detectors, FPRs for human-written texts varied widely, from 0.0% to 100.0%. Responses varied across detectors: the same edits increased AI scores in some detectors but decreased them in others. Notably, score changes correlated with the extent of editing. The findings identify professional editing style as a key confounding variable in AI detector outputs, rather than establishing a full separation of text origin from linguistic style, raising concerns about fairness and reliability in academic settings.

</details>


### [264] [Knowing When Not to Reuse: Conditional Experience Transfer in Autonomous LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26730)
*Tingyun Li,Wenfeng Feng,Weiqing Li,Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi,Guohua Liu,Yuewei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将"经验授权"(experience authorization)作为自主后训练中的一个独立问题。为解决"过去更新证据在父模型变更后是否仍然可用"这一核心难题，作者引入边界标定干预迁移(BCIT)方法：将观测到的效果绑定到其源上下文，检查适用条件，否决存在命名硬冲突的候选，并在必要时通过有界训练试验获取当前状态证据。在4B模型跨金融推理、text-to-SQL和函数调用三个场景的实验中，BCIT在相同候选、证据与算力预算下，比对照方法授权更少的有害更新，并取得更高的等预算最终模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型功能强大，但适应不断演化的领域、工具和需求通常需要重复的后期训练。自主系统通过提出更新、训练候选以及用评估反馈选择下一方案来自动化这一流程的一部分。随着证据累积，一个核心问题浮现：当后续训练改变了父模型之后，哪些过去的更新证据仍然可执行？一次更新的效果取决于其父模型、数据和训练阶段。把过去的成功当作无上下文限制的许可会浪费算力；若由此产生的子模型被晋升，还会恶化后续训练轨迹。作者将这一问题形式化为"条件经验迁移"，并指出经验授权值得作为一个专门的科学问题来研究，而非简单地把历史成功当作通用许可。

Method: 作者提出边界标定干预迁移(BCIT)方法：将观测到的效果与其来源上下文绑定，检查适用条件，对带有命名硬冲突的候选行使否决权，并在需要时通过一次有界训练试验获取当前状态的证据。完整训练的候选仍须遵循共享的采纳规则，且只有被观测到的事件才能扩展记忆（即先验授权、试验验证、观测记忆三阶段的闭环）。该方法在权重改变型训练之前进行授权，从而在源上下文信息中界定"何时可复用经验"的边界。

Result: 在4B模型上跨金融推理、text-to-SQL和函数调用三个场景进行适配实验，候选更新的目标效应与保留效应在不同上下文中呈现异质性。在候选、证据和算力均匹配的条件下，BCIT相比所评估的替代方法授权了更少的有害更新，并在同等算力预算下达到更高的最终模型质量。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，应当将经验授权视为自主后训练中一个独立于训练本身的问题。只基于历史成功的无上下文复用是有风险的，BCIT这类将效果绑定到上下文并进行有界验证的机制能更有效地利用过去的更新证据，从而为未来的自主后训练系统设计提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models offer broad capabilities, but adapting them to evolving domains, tools, and requirements often entails repeated post-training. Autonomous systems automate parts of this process by proposing updates, training candidates, and using evaluation feedback to select subsequent proposals. As evidence accumulates, a central problem emerges: which past update evidence remains actionable after subsequent training has changed the parent model? An update's effect depends on its parent, data, and training stage. Treating past success as context-free permission can waste compute. If the resulting child is promoted, it can also degrade the subsequent training trajectory. We formulate this problem as conditional experience transfer and introduce Boundary-Calibrated Intervention Transfer (BCIT), a method that authorizes experience reuse before weight-changing training. BCIT binds an observed effect to its source context, checks applicability conditions, vetoes candidates with named hard conflicts, and obtains current-state evidence through a bounded training trial when needed. Fully trained candidates still face a shared adoption rule, and only observed events extend memory. On one 4B model adapted across finance reasoning, text-to-SQL, and function calling, candidate updates exhibit heterogeneous target and retention effects across the evaluated contexts. Under matched candidates, evidence, and compute, BCIT authorizes fewer harmful updates and attains higher equal-budget final-model quality than the evaluated alternatives. These results support treating experience authorization as a distinct problem in autonomous post-training.

</details>


### [265] [Graph-Guided Selective Unlearning for Language Models: Controlling Support Routes Beyond Forget Seeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26743)
*Waqas Khan,Tabinda Sarwar,Jingyue Cong,Xun Yi,Estrid He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GRAPHSU，一个图引导的控制器，用于扩展选择性遗忘（selective unlearning）的删除范围。不同于现有方法只处理明确标记的“遗忘种子”（forget seeds），GRAPHSU 通过构建带权重的“支持-路线图”（support-route graph），沿图传播删除压力，并对高风险的相邻样本施加分级遗忘强度，从而防止目标知识经由改写（paraphrases）、别名（aliases）或邻近训练样本被重新恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在大模型上微调专有数据后，可能因隐私、合同或合规要求需要删除部分数据。选择性遗忘提供了一种比全量重训更实用的方案，但现有方法把显式标识的遗忘样本当作完全的删除范围，这并不充分——目标知识仍可通过改写、别名或邻近训练样本被恢复，导致“软泄漏”（soft leakage）。

Method: GRAPHSU 的核心是：1) 构建一个带权重的支持-路线图，将遗忘种子与其支持性、邻近的样本关联起来，刻画知识可被恢复的传播路径；2) 通过该图传播删除压力，识别高风险的相邻样本；3) 对高风险邻居施加分级（graded）遗忘强度，从而把删除范围从遗忘种子扩展到整个支持路线，而不仅仅是种子本身。

Result: 在 TOFU（虚构作者问答基准）和 PISTOL（面向互联事实样本的结构性遗忘基准）上，配合 GPT-2 Medium 与 Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct，GRAPHSU 在所有删除设置下实现了“效用可行范围内最低的软泄漏”，相比匹配的仅种子基线，泄漏最多降低 49.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 有效的企业级遗忘不仅需要控制“遗忘种子”，还需要控制支撑这些知识的传播路线（support routes）。GRAPHSU 验证了图引导的删除范围扩展能显著降低知识可恢复性，同时维持模型效用，为选择性遗忘提供了更稳健的实践方案。

Abstract: Enterprises fine-tune language models on proprietary data that may later require removal due to privacy, contractual, or compliance obligations. Selective unlearning removes requested knowledge while preserving model utility, offering a practical alternative to full retraining, but existing methods treat the explicitly identified forget examples as the complete deletion scope. This is insufficient when target knowledge remains recoverable through paraphrases, aliases, or neighboring training examples. We propose GRAPHSU, a graph-guided controller that expands the deletion scope beyond forget seeds by constructing a weighted support-route graph, propagating deletion pressure through it, and applying graded forgetting strengths to high-risk neighbors. On the Task of Fictitious Unlearning (TOFU), a synthetic author-profile question-answering benchmark, and PISTOL, a structural-unlearning benchmark built around interconnected factual samples, with GPT-2 Medium and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, GRAPHSU achieves the lowest utility-feasible soft leakage across all deletion settings, reducing leakage by up to 49.5 percentage points over a matched seed-only baseline, demonstrating that effective enterprise unlearning requires controlling support routes, not just forget seeds.

</details>


### [266] [AgentFold: Closed-Loop Agentic Search for Protein Folding Model Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26747)
*Mingquan Liu,Jiangyu Chen,Hanqun Cao,Xujun Zhang,Pengsen Ma,Xiangru Tang,Shuting Jin,Zhuo Yang,Tianfan Fu,Fang Wu,Xiangxiang Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentFold，一个多智能体框架，将蛋白质折叠模型开发形式化为对可执行代码变体的闭环搜索。从 ESMFold 出发，AgentFold 自主提出假设、实现并调试代码级修改、评估变体模型并分析实验结果，同时将成功与失败的干预记录存入结构化记忆。在超过 2,000 行代码的工程规模代码库上，AgentFold 探索了约 80 个模型变体，花费约 5,000 GPU 小时和 1.7 亿 LLM token；在匹配的计算预算下，相比独立 Codex 提案，最佳 lDDT 提升 7.5%，并优于随机搜索基线。干预轨迹揭示了可复现的设计规律：稳定增益来自早期、柔和、可学习的先验与门控精化，而直接几何扰动和几何条件反馈常破坏训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学 LLM 智能体已在文献推理、工具使用和实验规划方面展现出潜力，但仍不清楚它们能否通过可执行代码修改和昂贵计算验证，自主改进大型、紧耦合的科学机器学习系统。蛋白质折叠正是此类系统：模型发展需要协调的架构修改、多目标评估和领域感知解释。作者旨在探索 LLM 智能体能否在这一高难度场景中实现真正的自主代码级研发闭环。

Method: AgentFold 采用多智能体框架，将折叠模型开发构建为对可执行代码变体的闭环搜索。流程包括：提出假设、实现与调试代码级修改、评估模型变体、分析实验结果，并将成功与失败的干预存入结构化记忆。一个基于 MCTS 的策略负责在高分搜索分支间分配计算资源，从而在有限预算内引导搜索向有前景的方向推进。

Result: 在超过 2,000 行的蛋白质折叠代码库上，AgentFold 探索了约 80 个模型变体，消耗约 5,000 GPU 小时和 1.7 亿 LLM token。在匹配的计算预算下，AgentFold 将最佳 lDDT 相比独立 Codex 提案提升 7.5%，并优于随机搜索对照。此外，干预轨迹揭示了可复现的经验设计模式：稳定增益源于早期、柔和、可学习的先验和门控精化，而直接几何扰动与几何条件反馈往往导致训练不稳定。

Conclusion: AgentFold 证明 LLM 智能体能够自主地通过代码修改和计算验证改进复杂的科学机器学习系统，而不仅仅是做推理或规划。其记录的干预轨迹提炼出了具有可迁移性的领域设计准则，为将自动化搜索用于科学模型研发提供了可行范式。代码与实验资源已公开。

Abstract: Scientific LLM agents have shown promise in literature reasoning, tool use, and experiment planning, but it remains unclear whether they can autonomously improve large, tightly coupled scientific machine-learning systems through executable code changes and computationally expensive validation. We study this question in protein folding, where progress requires coordinated architectural modifications, multi-objective evaluation, and domain-aware interpretation. We present AgentFold, a multi-agent framework that formulates folding-model development as a closed-loop search over executable code variants. Starting from ESMFold, AgentFold proposes hypotheses, implements and debugs code-level modifications, evaluates model variants, analyzes experimental outcomes, and stores both successful and failed interventions in structured memory. An MCTS-style policy allocates computational resources across high-scoring search branches. On an engineering-scale protein-folding codebase comprising more than 2,000 lines of code, AgentFold explores approximately 80 model variants using approximately 5,000 GPU-hours and 170 million LLM tokens. Under a matched computational budget, AgentFold improves the best lDDT by 7.5% over independent Codex proposals and outperforms a random-search control. Beyond model improvement, the resulting intervention traces reveal recurring empirical design patterns: stable gains tend to arise from early, soft, learnable priors and gated refinement, whereas direct geometric perturbations and geometry-conditioned feedback often destabilize training. The code and experimental resources are publicly available at https://github.com/lmqfly/AgentFold.

</details>


### [267] [Discovering Relationships in Data Lakes Using Large Language Models: An Industrial Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26750)
*Ahlame Diouan,Eric Ferey,Sabine Loudcher,Jérôme Darmont*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ColRel，一种两阶段方法，利用元数据和数据在摄入时构建列嵌入，用于数据湖中的列关系发现；在编码/缩写的模式标签（如 ERP 数据集）等困难场景下，借助业务词典解释列名并生成简短的天然语言描述，以增强第二阶段的效果。实验表明 ColRel 在语义相关、弱信号场景中尤为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖的可用性依赖元数据，但元数据常常有限或对列关系发现的信息量不足，尤其是在 ERP 衍生数据集中，模式标签常为编码或缩写形式，导致基于列名的关系发现效果不佳。

Method: ColRel 采用两阶段方法：第一阶段从摄入时的元数据与数据构建列嵌入；第二阶段利用业务词典解释编码的列名，并生成简短的天然语言描述来辅助列关系判定，从而应对模式编码等困难情形。

Result: 在公开基准数据集和工业 ERP 数据集上的实验表明，ColRel 在语义相关的弱信号场景下表现尤为有效。

Conclusion: ColRel 通过结合元数据、数据与业务词典，能有效提升数据湖中列关系发现的性能，特别适用于编码模式标签、信号较弱的实际工业数据环境。

Abstract: Data lakes rely on metadata to remain usable, yet this meta data is often limited or weakly informative for column relationship discovery, especially in ERP-derived datasets with coded or abbreviated schema labels. We propose ColRel, a two-stage method that builds column embeddings from metadata and data available at ingestion time. In difficult cases, such as coded schemata, business dictionaries help better interpret column names and support the generation of short natural-language descriptions used in the second stage. Experiments on public benchmarks and an industrial ERP dataset show that ColRel is particularly effective in semantically related, weak-signal settings.

</details>


### [268] [DEEPCHART: How Far are LLMs from Faithful Data-Science Chart Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26757)
*Jiahui tang,Kuicai Dong,Dexun Li,Hongchao Gu,Haocheng Yu,Wei Han,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DEEPCHART，一个包含1,482个任务条件化图表生成实例的专家标注基准，将图表生成形式化为“抽取—推理—可视化”流水线，并逐阶段评估源数据抽取、派生数据推理和图表渲染。实验表明，视觉上合理的图表常掩盖数据层面的幻觉，抽取得分与推理错误在长上下文、多模态的真实场景中普遍存在，说明仅靠更大的上下文窗口不足以实现忠实的图表生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据科学工作流中的忠实图表生成要求将可视化建立在零散证据之上、计算可直接制表的数据并准确渲染。现代LLM能生成视觉上合理且符合指令的图表，但数据层面的幻觉在长且嘈杂的多模态上下文中难以检测，现有评估缺乏对该差距的度量。

Method: 构建DEEPCHART基准，实例来自真实科学论文、金融文件和生态系统报告，经专家标注。将图表生成定义为Extract–Reason–Visualize流水线，分别对源数据抽取、派生数据推理和图表渲染三个阶段进行评估。

Result: 对最新模型的实验显示，视觉上合理的图表往往掩盖数据层面幻觉，在现实长上下文与多模态设定中，抽取和推理错误常见。

Conclusion: 更大上下文窗口本身不足以保证忠实图表生成；在渲染之前还需要可靠的证据抽取与定量推理。基准与相关资源已在GitHub公开。

Abstract: Faithful chart generation in real-world data-science workflows requires grounding visualizations in scattered evidence, computing chart-ready quantities, and rendering them accurately. Modern LLMs can produce visually plausible, instruction-compliant charts, yet data-level hallucinations remain difficult to detect in long, noisy, and multimodal contexts. To measure this gap, we introduce DEEPCHART, an expert-annotated benchmark of 1,482 task-conditioned chart-generation instances drawn from real-world scientific papers, financial filings, and ecosystem reports. DEEPCHART formulates chart generation as an Extract--Reason--Visualize pipeline and evaluates source-data extraction, derived-data reasoning, and chart rendering stage by stage. Experiments with state-of-the-art models show that visually plausible charts often conceal data-level hallucinations, with extraction and reasoning errors common in realistic long and multimodal settings. These findings suggest that larger context windows alone are insufficient; faithful chart generation also requires reliable evidence extraction and quantitative reasoning before rendering. Our benchmark and associated resources are available at https://github.com/tangdouer1005/DeepChart.

</details>


### [269] [Categorizer Automata for Discounted-Sum Payoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26763)
*Nathalie Bertrand,Pranav Ghorpade,Senthil Rajasekaran,Sasha Rubin,Moshe Vardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"分类器自动机"（categorizer automaton），一种确定性自动机，通过读取无限长度的奖励序列，判断其折扣和（discounted sum）落入有限个区间（bin）中的哪一个。该模型推广了已有的比较器自动机（两个区间的情形），并给出了状态数关于区间数目呈线性增长的构造（而非通过比较器自动机叉积得到的指数级）。作者将其应用于马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），用于合成在可能不连续的效用函数下最大化折扣和收益期望效用的策略：对分段常数效用函数，算法精确且运行于伪多项式时间；对分段Lipschitz效用函数，同样在伪多项式时间内给出ε-最优策略。最后证明即使对分段常数效用，该综合问题已是PSPACE难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能中，将连续数据离散化为区间是基本操作。已有的比较器自动机（仅支持两个区间）已在定量综合中证明有用，但处理多个区间的自然做法——比较器自动机做叉积——会导致状态空间指数级膨胀。因此需要一种能高效处理任意有限多个区间、且状态数随区间数线性增长的自动机模型，以支撑更广泛的（尤其是效用函数可能不连续的）定量综合问题。

Method: 论文的核心技术贡献是构造分类器自动机：它读取无限奖励序列，直接判定折扣和落入哪个区间，其状态空间关于区间数量为线性，避免了多个比较器自动机叉积带来的指数爆炸。在此基础上，将分类器自动机嵌入MDP的定量综合框架：将原优化问题转化为在乘积结构上求解，从而合成最大化折扣和期望效用的策略。针对分段常数效用函数给出精确算法，针对分段Lipschitz效用函数给出ε-最优算法，二者均为伪多项式时间。

Result: 构造出状态数线性于区间数的分类器自动机；给出基于该自动机的MDP策略综合算法：分段常数效用下算法精确且为伪多项式时间；分段Lipschitz效用（含任意有界斜率、有限跳变）下同样伪多项式时间并产生ε-最优策略。另外证明即使对分段常数效用，所考虑的综合问题已是PSPACE难。

Conclusion: 分类器自动机为多区间折扣和分类提供了一种结构紧凑（线性状态）、理论上界明确（伪多项式时间算法，PSPACE难下界）的框架，它将比较器自动机的成果推广到任意有限多个区间，并使得MDP中针对不连续效用函数的策略综合变得可行且高效。

Abstract: Categorizing continuous data into discrete bins is a fundamental operation in artificial intelligence. We introduce the categorizer automaton, a deterministic automaton that reads an infinite sequence of rewards and identifies which of finitely many bins contains its discounted sum. Categorizer automata generalize comparator automata, the special case of two bins, which have already proven useful in quantitative synthesis. Our main technical contribution is the construction of a categorizer automaton whose state space is linear in the number of bins, rather than exponential as obtained by a cross-product of comparator automata. We then apply categorizer automata to Markov decision processes, where they allow one to synthesize policies that maximize the expected utility of a discounted-sum payoff for utility functions that may be discontinuous. For piecewise-constant utility functions, the resulting algorithm is exact and runs in pseudo-polynomial time. For piecewise-Lipschitz utility functions, a class that includes any utility with bounded slope between finitely many jumps, it again runs in pseudo-polynomial time and yields an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy. We also show that the synthesis problem considered is PSPACE-hard already for piecewise-constant utilities.

</details>


### [270] [AI Control Scientist: LLM-driven Agentic System for Automated Control Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26780)
*Haiteng Wang,Weihao Li,Jing Zhang,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AI Control Scientist (AICS)，这是首个由大语言模型（LLM）驱动的智能体系统，能够根据语言设计需求自动生成优化的控制系统，将传统依赖专家经验与人工参数整定的控制设计流程转变为智能体驱动的自动化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制系统设计（如化工过程温度调节、航空发动机控制）对现代工业至关重要，但传统控制设计工作流高度依赖专家知识和大量人工参数整定，导致效率低下、可扩展性有限。因此需要一种能够自动从语言需求出发生成控制系统的自动化方法。

Method: AICS 由三个协同工作的智能体组成：任务建模智能体（Task Modeling Agent）将用户需求解释为工程约束；控制器设计智能体（Controller Design Agent）生成候选控制器结构和可执行代码；参数整定智能体（Parameter Tuning Agent）在闭环性能准则下优化控制器参数。三者构成完整的自动化设计流水线。

Result: 实验表明，该智能体系统能自动生成多种代表性控制系统，并在设计成功率和优化效率两方面均优于现有自动化基线方法。

Conclusion: 这项工作有望将控制系统设计从人驱动转变为智能体驱动，为模型预测控制及其他先进控制系统的设计铺平道路。

Abstract: Control system design is critical for modern industry, such as chemical process temperature regulation and aero-engine control. However,traditional control design workflows rely heavily on expert knowledge and extensive manual parameter tuning, resulting in limited efficiency and scalability. To this end, this paper proposes AI Control Scientist (AICS), the first large language model (LLM)-driven agent capable of automatically generating optimized controller from language design requirements. Specifically, a Task Modeling Agent interprets user requirements to engineering constraints; a Controller Design Agent generate candidate controller structures and executable code; and a Parameter Tuning Agent refine controller parameters under closed-loop performance criteria. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed agentic system can automatically generate multiple representative control systems, outperforms existing automated baselines in both design success rate and optimization efficiency. This work has the potential to transform control system design from human-driven to agent-driven, paving the way for model predictive control and other advanced control systems design.

</details>


### [271] [Decoupling Planning and Control for Instructable Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26788)
*Zineng Tang,Kelsey R. Allen,Sjoerd van Steenkiste,Ishita Dasgupta,Alane Suhr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Instruct-to-Act，将擅长高层规划的 VLM 规划器（低频、稀疏文本指令）与擅长低延迟控制的 world-model 控制器结合为单一系统。控制器通过在策略 rollout 片段上添加合成语言指令并联合优化模仿学习、奖励最大化和世界建模目标来获得语言可控性。在七个具身环境（含三个多智能体环境）的评测中，该解耦方法在匹配观测与动作空间下稳定优于控制器单独使用和直接 VLM 生成动作的变体，保持快速控制能力，可无微调地换用不同预训练 VLM 规划器，并在六/七个任务上与强基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练指令微调视觉语言模型（VLM）擅长依据指令和观测生成高层计划，但在陌生环境中难以将该计划落实为可靠的低延迟动作序列；而 world-model 控制器擅长快速地从观测映射到动作，却缺乏开放式的任务指引能力。两类方法的优缺点互补，因此作者希望将二者的优势整合到一个系统中。

Method: 构建 Instruct-to-Act 解耦系统：(1) VLM 规划器负责以较少频次生成稀疏的高层文本指令；(2) world-model 控制器以高频自主执行动作，并以该文本指令为条件。为让控制器具备语言可指示性，作者将控制器策略 rollout 的片段用合成指令重标注，并将行为克隆目标与现有奖励最大化、世界建模目标联合优化。

Result: 在七个具身环境（含三个 VLM 通过语言协调、控制器作为执行器的多智能体环境）中，该解耦方法在匹配观测与动作空间条件下一致优于 controller-only 和直接 VLM 动作生成变体，保留了快速控制特性，可在无需微调的情况下替换不同的预训练 VLM 规划器，并在六个任务上达到与强视觉-语言-动作及多智能体 RL 基线相当的水平。

Conclusion: 解耦的"高层语言规划 + 低层快速控制"架构能够兼顾开放式的任务指引与可靠的低延迟动作执行，且模块可替换、可扩展，为将 VLM 规划能力与学习型控制器结合提供了一种有效且实用的设计范式。

Abstract: Recent work shows that pre-trained, instruction-tuned vision-language models (VLMs) perform well at mapping from instructions and observations to high-level plans, but struggle to realize such plans as reliable low-latency action sequences in unfamiliar environments. At the same time, world-model controllers excel at fast observation-to-action control, but lack open-ended task guidance. In this work, we combine these strengths into a single system, Instruct-to-Act, where we train a world-model controller to act autonomously at high frequency when conditioned on sparse, higher-latency, and high-level text instructions generated by a VLM planner. To train controllers to be language-instructable, we relabel segments of controller policy rollouts with synthetic instructions and jointly optimize a behavior-cloning objective along with existing reward-maximizing and world-modeling objectives. We evaluate our proposed approach across seven embodied environments, including three multi-agent environments where VLM planners coordinate through language while trained controllers serve as their actuators. Under matched observation and action spaces, our decoupled approach consistently outperforms controller-only and direct VLM action-generation variants, preserves fast control, and lets us swap in different pretrained VLM planners without fine-tuning, while remaining competitive with strong vision-language-action and multi-agent RL baselines on six of seven tasks.

</details>


### [272] [SymbolLKG: Towards Verifiable Logical Reasoning via Logical Knowledge Graph and Symbolic Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26836)
*Haizhao Fan,Yuchi Xiong,Jize Wang,Xinping Guan,Xinyi Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号架构，将逻辑知识图谱（LKG）与动态求解器路由相结合，以解决LLM在严格多步推理中的幻觉和不一致问题，在逻辑推理基准上显著优于SOTA的提示与RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽具备出色的自然语言理解能力，但在严格的多步推理中经常出现幻觉和不一致；CoT缺乏严谨的验证机制，标准RAG忽略了逻辑任务中复杂的结构化依赖关系，现有方案难以弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出Neuro-Symbolic架构：(1) 基于本体的逻辑知识图谱（LKG），将逻辑规则和约束作为"一等拓扑节点"，显式建模文本中提取出的依赖关系；(2) 逻辑路由（Logic Router）动态将任务分派给最优符号引擎；(3) 支持拓扑感知的混合检索机制。

Result: 在逻辑推理基准上的实验表明，该框架在准确率和可验证推理路径两方面显著优于SOTA的提示（prompting）与RAG基线。

Conclusion: 将符号引擎的严谨性与LLM的理解能力相结合，并通过可验证的推理路径提升可靠性与一致性，为逻辑推理任务提供了一种可行且有效的神经符号解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in natural language understanding, yet they struggle with strict multi-step reasoning, frequently suffering from hallucinations and inconsistency. Existing solutions like Chain-of-Thought (CoT) lack rigorous verification mechanisms, while standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) often misses the complex, structural dependencies inherent in logical tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose a Neuro-Symbolic architecture that integrates a Logical Knowledge Graph (LKG) with dynamic solver routing. Specifically, we introduce an ontology-based LKG that treats logical rules and constraints as first-class topological nodes, enabling explicit modeling of dependencies extracted from text. We further design a Logic Router to dynamically dispatch tasks to the optimal symbolic engine, which is supported by a topology-aware hybrid retrieval mechanism. Experimental results on logical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art prompting and RAG baselines, delivering higher accuracy and verifiable reasoning paths.

</details>


### [273] [LiveSim: Simulating Environment-Shaped Users in Multi-Agent Live-Stream Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26849)
*Jiaqi Xu,Yiran Qiao,Jing Chen,Qiwei Zhong,Xiang Ao,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LiveSim，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的直播生态系统仿真框架。它把用户表示为可编辑的行为假设，并通过轨迹实体的交互逐步修正这些假设；模拟轨迹与观测轨迹之间的差异被提取为可迁移的环境-行为模式，积累到集体行为记忆中，从而提升用户级行为保真度并支持生态系统级仿真。在真实直播风控数据上的实验验证了其在用户级行为保真度和风险演化、平台干预效果分析上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的用户行为模拟器通常依赖从历史观测中推断出的静态用户画像，但在直播这类高社交密集环境中，交互动态会持续重塑用户行为，静态画像难以刻画这种动态变化，导致模拟保真度不足。

Method: 1) 将用户表示为可编辑的行为假设（editable behavioral hypotheses）；2) 通过轨迹实体的交互（trajectory-grounded interactions）逐步细化这些假设，其中模拟轨迹与观测轨迹的差异用于揭示缺失的环境塑造效应；3) 将这些信号提取为可迁移的环境-行为模式（environment-behavior patterns），并积累在集体行为记忆（collective behavioral memory）中；4) 以该记忆改进用户级行为保真度，并支持生态系统级仿真。

Result: 在真实直播风控数据上的实验表明，LiveSim能够提升用户级行为保真度，并支持生态系统级分析，包括风险演化分析以及平台干预效果的评估。

Conclusion: LiveSim通过将环境塑造效应纳入行为建模，有效弥补了静态用户画像在社交密集型环境中的不足，为直播生态系统仿真及风险控制提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: User behavior simulation with large language models~(LLMs) is increasingly used to support multi-agent ecosystem simulation. Existing simulators typically rely on static user profiles inferred from historical observations, which become inadequate in socially intensive environments such as live streaming where interaction dynamics continuously reshape user behavior. We propose \textbf{LiveSim}, an LLM-based framework for live-stream ecosystem simulation. It represents users as editable behavioral hypotheses and progressively refines them through trajectory-grounded interactions, where discrepancies between simulated and observed trajectories reveal missing environmental shaping effects. These signals are further extracted as transferable environment-behavior patterns and accumulated in a collective behavioral memory to improve user-level behavioral fidelity and support ecosystem-level simulation. Experiments on real-world live-stream risk-control data validate the effectiveness of LiveSim in improving user-level behavioral fidelity and enabling ecosystem-level analysis of risk evolution and platform intervention effects.

</details>


### [274] [BekchiAI: Measuring, Observing, and Controlling LLM Agents in One Click](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26867)
*Mesut Toruk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BekchiAI，包含两部分：一个用于测量 LLM 智能体“智能体技能”（如工具排序、依赖规划、不可信输入判断、基于来源的论证）的基准测试，以及一个用于观测和控制线上智能体的可观测性平台。基准含 13 个使用 ReAct 的智能体、7 类任务、2057 个确定性测试任务，所有答案均可验证；平台提供完整 token 与延迟遥测及远程终止能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体具备多步推理、工具调用与自主行动能力，但其智能体技能（正确排序工具、在依赖关系下规划、判断不可信输入、对生成论证进行来源 grounding）难以用仅看准确率的排行榜来衡量，需要一个针对性基准和可控观测平台。

Method: 构建 BekchiAI-Benchmark：13 个工具型 ReAct 智能体，覆盖 7 类任务（算术、结构化/SQL、安全检测、URL grounding、规划、编排、工具策略），共 2057 个确定性且已提交的测试任务。每个任务均有可验证的金标准——通过运行规范 SQL 查询真实数据库、计算有向无环图(DAG)的精确调度、或计算闭式 lambda 求得；含对抗性安全样本并配以刻意不完善的签名扫描器，使得分反映模型自身判断而非复制 oracle。定义超越准确率的行为指标：工具调用一致性、URL 幻觉与来源匹配率、各模型 token 成本；通过提供的评测脚本执行并报告四模型对比（Qwen3.7-Max、gemma-4-31B-it、gemma4:26b、gpt-oss-120b），强调按家族内差异而非聚合值解读。BekchiAI-Platform 为部署智能体提供 Web 可观测性与控制层，含完整 token/延迟遥测和远程终止运行。

Result: 给出四项行为指标上的四模型对比结果，关键信息蕴含在各模型家族内部的指标差异中，而非聚合准确率；基准、评测工具与平台均已公开。

Conclusion: BekchiAI 同时补齐了智能体技能评测与线上智能体观测/控制两个缺口，通过可验证任务与行为级指标，能更真实地反映模型自身的判断与规划能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents reason, call tools, and act autonomously over many steps, but their agentic skills-correctly sequencing tools, planning under dependencies, judging untrusted inputs, and grounding generated arguments-are hard to measure with accuracy-only leaderboards. We present BekchiAI, which addresses both sides: a benchmark for measuring agentic skill and a platform for observing and controlling live agents. The BekchiAI-Benchmark, a suite of 13 tool-using ReAct agents across 7 task categories (arithmetic, structured/SQL, security detection, URL grounding, planning, orchestration, and tool-policy), totalling 2,057 deterministic, committed test tasks. Every task is verifier-checkable gold answers are computed by running canonical SQL against a real database, computing the exact schedule of a directed acyclic graph (DAG), or evaluating closed-form lambdas including adversarial security samples paired with deliberately imperfect signature scanners so a score reflects the model's own judgment, not the copying of an oracle. We define a small set of behavioral metrics beyond accuracy-tool-call adherence, URL hallucination and source-match, and per-model token cost and report a four-model comparison (Qwen3.7-Max, gemma-4-31B-it, gemma4:26b, gpt-oss-120b) whose story is in the per-family spread, not the aggregate. The benchmark runs are executed using the provided evaluation scripts. BekchiAI-Platform is a complementary web-based observability and control layer for deployed agents, providing full token and latency telemetry as well as remote run termination. The benchmark, evaluation tools, and platform are publicly released.

</details>


### [275] [C-Unseen: Weak Signal Detection in Dynamic Temporal Knowledge Graphs via LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26870)
*Yassir Lairgi,Ludovic Moncla,Khalid Benabdeslem,Rémy Cazabet,Pierre Cléau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 C-Unseen，一个用于动态时序知识图谱（DTKG）中弱信号检测的自可解释框架。弱信号被定义为在连续 TKG 快照中增殖的罕见且语义连贯的子图，通过 LLM 抽取罕见子图并追踪其跨时间步的持续性来识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱信号是重大变化确立前的早期、低可见性指标。现有方法基于关键词频率、主题建模或未类型化图拓扑，无法捕捉弱信号赖以实现表达的语义与关系结构，因此检测能力受限。

Method: C-Unseen 由两个模块构成：1) 罕见子图抽取器（Rare Subgraphs Extractor），利用 LLM 通过链式推理识别与主导快照叙事相悖的内容子图；2) 弱信号警报器（Weak Signal Alerter），跨时间步追踪这些罕见子图的持续性，从而筛出真正的弱信号。

Result: 实验结果表明，C-Unseen 在检测性能上优于基于关键词、主题和图的基线方法。

Conclusion: 将弱信号形式化为"跨快照增殖的罕见语义连贯子图"，并结合 LLM 语义推理与时间持续性追踪，能够有效捕获弱信号，为该任务提供了一条自可解释的检测路径。

Abstract: Weak signals are early, low-visibility indicators that precede significant changes before those changes become established. Existing detection methods, based on keyword frequency, topic modeling, or untyped graph topology, fail to capture the semantic and relational structure through which such signals manifest. In this paper, we propose C-Unseen, a self-interpretable framework for weak signal detection in Dynamic Temporal Knowledge Graphs (DTKGs). We define a weak signal as a rare, semantically coherent subgraph that proliferates across consecutive TKG snapshots. The framework operates through two modules: a Rare Subgraphs Extractor, in which an LLM identifies subgraphs whose content is in tension with the dominant snapshot narrative via chain-of-thought reasoning, and a Weak Signal Alerter, in which the persistence of these rare subgraphs is tracked across time steps to isolate true weak signals. Experimental results demonstrate that C-Unseen outperforms keyword-, topic-, and graph-based baselines.

</details>


### [276] [Evaluating human and LLM screening workflows in a conceptually complex scoping review: Recall--workload trade-offs and run-to-run consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26885)
*Nikol Figalová,Lynn Huestegge,Anne Böckler-Raettig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在一项预注册、嵌入概念复杂范围综述的研究中，比较了人类与LLM的标题-摘要筛选流程：人类流程和两个GPT-5.4文件批量运行在保留42.2-45.0%记录的同时达到约82.3-82.9%的操作召回率；Gemini 3.1文件批量的召回率最高（83.9%）但保留了56.7%的记录；所有流程均未找回全部已验证的合格记录，且两次名义上相同的GPT-5.4运行在94条记录上存在分歧（含29条已验证合格记录）。结论是LLM筛选性能取决于工作流实现而非仅模型本身，高召回任务中LLM更适合受监督、可审计的流程而非自主排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于证据综合中的筛选环节，而假阴性可能在被排除的研究进入全文评估前就将其漏掉。现有证据对LLM筛选性能的评估往往只关注模型本身，忽略了工作流配置的影响。作者希望通过在概念复杂的范围综述中嵌入预注册研究，严谨地比较人类与LLM筛选工作流，以澄清LLM在高召回任务中的实际适用性。

Method: 采用预注册的对比研究设计：先进行保守的标题层面筛选，得到1,131条记录；随后由1名综述负责人、4名训练有素的助理（各筛非重叠子集）以及7次完整的LLM运行（采用不同模型和处理配置，含一次名义上相同的重复运行）分别筛选。比较指标包括：保留工作量、针对316条已验证合格记录的操作召回率、一致性、运行间稳定性及流程负担。由于仅在父综述中推进并评估的记录才验证了合格性，召回率为操作性的估计。

Result: 没有任何工作流找回全部已验证的合格记录。人类工作流与两个GPT-5.4文件批量运行保留了42.2–45.0%的记录，召回率为82.3–82.9%；Gemini 3.1文件批量的召回率最高（83.9%），但保留了56.7%的记录；"一次性全部处理"（all-at-once）配置找回的合格记录少于对应的文件批量配置；两次名义上相同的GPT-5.4文件批量运行在91.7%的记录上一致，但在94条记录上出现分歧，其中29条为仅由一次运行保留的已验证合格记录。

Conclusion: LLM筛选性能取决于所实施的工作流，而非仅由模型身份决定；处理配置、工作量、记录级差异以及人机决策整合都是已部署系统的重要属性。对于高召回需求的任务，LLM更适合用于经过验证、可审计且有人类监督的工作流，而非自主排除记录。

Abstract: Background. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for screening in evidence synthesis, where false negatives can remove relevant studies before full-text assessment. We compared human and LLM title-and-abstract screening workflows in a preregistered study embedded in a conceptually complex scoping review.
  Methods. After a conservative title-only screen, 1,131 records were screened by one review lead, four trained assistants screening non-overlapping subsets, and seven complete LLM runs using different models and processing configurations, including a nominally identical repeat run. We compared retained workload, operational recall against 316 verified eligible records, agreement, run-to-run consistency, and procedural burden. Because eligibility was verified only for records advanced and assessed in the parent review, recall estimates were operational.
  Results. No workflow recovered all verified eligible records. The human workflows and two GPT-5.4 file-batch runs retained 42.2-45.0% of records while achieving 82.3-82.9% recall. Gemini 3.1 file batches achieved the highest recall (83.9%) but retained 56.7% of records. All-at-once configurations recovered fewer eligible records than corresponding file-batch configurations. Two nominally identical GPT-5.4 file-batch runs agreed on 91.7% of records but differed on 94 records, including 29 verified eligible records retained by only one run.
  Discussion. LLM screening performance depended on the implemented workflow, not model identity alone. Processing configuration, workload, record-level variation, and human-LLM decision integration are therefore substantive properties of deployed systems. For high-recall tasks, LLMs are better suited to validated, auditable, human-supervised workflows than autonomous exclusion.

</details>


### [277] [Learning-Augmented Online Allocation under Unreliable Advice: Robustness, Exposure Fairness, and Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26889)
*Fredy Pokou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种学习增强的在线分配算法，将不可靠的预测建议与保守回退策略及公平性修正相结合，在候选集有限、决策不可逆且存在曝光约束的场景下，实现了有界误差假设下与预测误差成比例的稳健性和一致性，并通过实验验证其在对抗性建议下保持稳定并显著降低曝光差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习增强算法利用预测来改善在线决策，但不可靠的建议可能损害效率和公平性。现有工作较少同时考虑有限候选集、不可逆决策和曝光约束下的鲁棒性与公平性保证，因此需要设计既能利用预测优势又能抵御不良建议的方案。

Method: 提出一种结合三要素的分配规则：(1) 利用预测建议以提高决策效率；(2) 引入保守回退机制以应对不可靠建议；(3) 加入公平性修正以控制曝光差异。研究在有界误差假设下对算法进行一致性（consistency）与鲁棒性（robustness）的理论分析，并通过实验验证其性能。

Result: 理论上证明了在有界误差假设下，算法保持一致性和鲁棒性，且损失与预测误差成比例；实验结果表明，算法在对抗性建议下仍能保持稳定，并显著降低了曝光不均衡（exposure disparity）。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过保守回退与公平性修正的合理设计，学习增强算法能够在享受预测收益的同时，有效抵御不可靠建议对效率与公平性的负面影响，为在线分配中的稳健公平决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms improve online decisions using predictions, but unreliable advice may harm efficiency and fairness. We study an online allocation problem with finite candidate sets, irreversible decisions, and exposure constraints. We propose a robust and fair rule combining advice with a conservative fallback and fairness correction. Under bounded-error assumptions, we prove consistency and robustness with loss proportional to prediction error. Experiments show stability under adversarial advice and significant reductions in exposure disparity.

</details>


### [278] [AI agents in Algorithmic Electricity Markets: On the Emergence of Tacit Collusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26896)
*Jakub Seredyński,Georgios Tsaousoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究电力市场中由自主学习算法（多智能体强化学习）控制的竞价行为是否会产生默契合谋（tacit collusion）。作者将策略性竞价建模为具有不完美公共监测的重复博弈，提出超越纳什均衡利润对比的多维合谋判定标准。实验表明，即使智能体从未被指示合谋，也可能学会维持超竞争性结果，证实电力市场存在默契合谋的现实风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三个动机：1) 电力市场参与方日益采用学习型智能体进行竞价，市场正变得算法化；2) 其他领域的算法市场已有证据表明纯粹通过独立学习即可产生默契合谋；3) 电力市场具有寡头垄断结构和少数参与者的重复互动特征，在结构上易受非竞争行为影响。因此需要检验"电力市场中由自主学习算法控制的参与者行为可能产生默契合谋"这一假设。

Method: 将策略性竞价建模为具有不完美公共监测（imperfect public monitoring）的重复博弈，用多智能体强化学习（multi-agent reinforcement learning）建模参与者的涌现行为。并提出一套多维判定标准（不局限于与纳什均衡的利润对比），用于评估所得行为是否构成默契合谋。

Result: 实验结果表明这种危险对电力市场而言是真实存在的：存在部分案例中智能体确实学会了维持超竞争性（supra-competitive）的结果，且这些结果支持默契合谋的判定指标，尽管智能体从未被指示去合谋。

Conclusion: 自主学习型竞价算法可能导致电力市场出现默契合谋，这一风险是现实而可验证的。研究强调了监管层面需要警惕算法化市场中的非竞争行为，并提供了超越传统纳什均衡比较的合谋识别方法。

Abstract: As electricity market participants increasingly adopt learning-based agents for their bidding strategies, electricity markets are becoming algorithmic. Evidence from algorithmic markets in other domains shows that tacit collusion can arise purely through independent learning. Moreover, electricity markets are typically oligopolistic and feature repeated interaction among a small number of participants, making them structurally susceptible to non-competitive behavior. In the face of these observations, this paper investigates the hypothesis that tacit collusion may emerge in electricity markets where participants' actions are controlled by autonomous learning-based algorithms. We model strategic bidding as a repeated game with imperfect public monitoring, and model the participants' emergent behavior using multi-agent reinforcement learning. We propose a multi-dimensional set of criteria (going beyond profit comparisons against Nash equilibria) to assess whether the resulting behavior constitutes tacit collusion. Our experimental results showcase that such a danger is realistic for electricity markets: there are cases where agents do learn to sustain supra-competitive outcomes that are supportive of tacit collusion indicators, even though the agents were never instructed to collude.

</details>


### [279] [Counterfactual Bias Testing for Application Tracking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26899)
*Sai Yashwant,Shruti Bansal,Anurag Dubey,Samaroha Chatterjee,Satyam Kumar,Shreyash Gupta,Gantala Thulsiram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM智能体生成简历的大型候选人-职位匹配管道审计方法：利用任务专用LLM合成身份中性简历并注入人口统计特征，通过嵌入模型排名和九指标公平性套件（反事实、群体公平、基于功绩三类）自动生成PASS/INVESTIGATE/FAIL报告与综合风险评分。在示例语料（5个职位、100名基线候选人、10种人口统计处理、90次指标×变体评估）上，得分偏移、top-K留存和功绩感知差距均在容差内，但MARC与nDCG@K分别暴露了仅凭得分或留存视角会忽略的边界问题（含中性基线自身一项）。结论主张多指标、多族系审计优于任何单一聚合分数，且LLM智能体生成的审计是人工审计的实用廉价补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动候选人-职位匹配系统在新兴法规下日益被归类为高风险AI，但对其进行人口统计偏见审计成本高昂：经典通信审计研究需要手工编写简历并手动提交，无法扩展到快速的管道重训练周期。

Method: 提出通用可复用方法：(1)用任务专用LLM智能体合成身份中性基线简历，并在五个受保护特征轴（性别、年龄、居住地、语言、残疾）注入受控人口统计处理，产生K×(1+N)通信审计矩阵；(2)按对齐EU AI法案的提示词定性标记推断的受保护特征；(3)通过微调句嵌入模型与余弦相似度按职位描述对候选人排名；(4)计算涵盖反事实（分数差、平均绝对排名变化、翻转率）、群体公平（top-K留存、五分之四/影响比率）与功绩感知（Recall@K、nDCG@K、机会均等、均等几率）三类共九项指标的公平套件，均含bootstrap置信区间、显著性检验与Benjamini-Hochberg校正，最终形成自动化PASS/INVESTIGATE/FAIL报告与复合风险评分。

Result: 在示例语料（5个职位订单、100名基线候选人、10种人口统计处理、90项指标×变体评估）上，所有处理的得分偏移、top-K留存和功绩感知率差均保持于容差内；但排名稳定性指标MARC与nDCG@K各自暴露出边界发现——包括中性基线自身的一项——而这些是得分或留存单一视角会遗漏的。

Conclusion: 结果支持应进行多指标、多族系审计而非依赖任何单一聚合分数，并表明LLM智能体生成的审计可作为人工策划审计的实用、低成本补充，适用于任何候选人-职位匹配管道。

Abstract: Automated candidate-job matching systems are increasingly classified as high-risk AI under emerging regulation, yet auditing them for demographic bias is expensive: classical correspondence-audit studies require hand-crafted resumes and manual submission, which does not scale to fast pipeline retraining cycles. This paper presents a general, reusable methodology that (1) uses task-specialized LLM agents to synthesize identity-neutral base resumes and inject controlled demographic treatments across five protected-characteristic axes (sex/gender, age, residence, language, disability), producing a K x (1+N) correspondence-audit matrix; (2) qualitatively flags inferred protected characteristics per an EU AI Act-aligned prompt; (3) ranks candidates against a job description via a fine-tuned sentence-embedding model and cosine similarity; and (4) computes a nine-metric fairness suite spanning counterfactual (score delta, mean absolute rank change, flip rate), group-fairness (top-K retention, four-fifths/impact ratio), and merit-aware (Recall@K, nDCG@K, equal opportunity, equalized odds) families, each with bootstrap confidence intervals, significance tests, and Benjamini-Hochberg correction, culminating in an automated PASS/INVESTIGATE/FAIL report with a composite risk score. On an example corpus of 5 job orders, 100 base candidates, and 10 demographic treatments (90 metric x variant evaluations): score shifts, top-K retention, and merit-aware rate gaps stay within tolerance for every treatment, but a rank-stability metric (MARC) and nDCG@K each surface borderline findings - including one on the neutral baseline itself - that a score- or retention-only view would miss. The results argue for multi-metric, multi-family auditing over any single aggregate score, and for LLM-agent-generated audits as a practical, low-cost complement to human-curated audits for any candidate-job matching pipeline.

</details>


### [280] [A Table Is Worth 64 Tokens: Pixel-level Compression for Multi-Table Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26949)
*Iñigo Alonso,Mirella Lapata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究面向文档表格问答的像素级（光学）上下文压缩。实验表明：表格以原生分辨率转成图像时，性能与效率均与文本相当；但缩小表格会迫使模型用更长且低效的推理链弥补可读性损失，抵消压缩带来的收益。不过，高度缩小的表格仍足以判断其是否与问题相关。作者利用这一不对称性提出无训练的“两步法”——先在像素压缩上下文中定位所需表格，再对原生分辨率表格推理——在长文档上节省41%总token并提升7个准确率点，且比最高效的单步压缩方案少用15% token且无精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实文档问答需要处理文本与表格交织的长输入，光学上下文压缩（将上下文表示为图像）可降低token成本，但其对表格理解的影响尚不清楚，因此作者系统研究像素级表格压缩下的多表格文档问答。

Method: 在5个VLM、2个基准和5种视觉token预算下进行系统性评测；基于“高倍缩小表格仍保留相关性信号、但牺牲可读性”这一发现，提出训练无关的两步法：第一步在像素压缩上下文中识别回答问题所需的表格，第二步对定位到的原生分辨率表格进行推理。

Result: 原生分辨率下的表格图像与文本在性能与效率上相当；缩小表格会引发更长但低效的推理痕迹，抵消预期节省。两步法在长文档上节省41%总token且准确率提升7点；相比最高效的单步压缩配置少用15% token且无精度损失。

Conclusion: 像素级压缩对表格问答存在“可读性”与“相关性判断”之间不对称的权衡；通过先粗粒度筛选、后细粒度推理的分解策略，可以在无需训练的情况下同时获得显著的token节省和精度提升，为光学上下文压缩在表格密集文档中的应用提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Answering questions over real-world documents requires processing long inputs that interleave text with tables. Optical context compression, which represents context as images, promises to reduce token cost, but its effect on table understanding remains unclear. We study pixel-level table compression for question answering over documents with multiple tables, evaluating five VLMs across two benchmarks and five visual-token budgets. Representing tables as images at native resolution matches text in both performance and efficiency, but downscaling them makes models compensate the loss in readability with longer, less effective reasoning traces that cancel the expected savings. Highly downscaled tables, however, preserve enough signal to identify whether they are relevant to a question. We exploit this asymmetry with a training-free, two-step method: the model first identifies the tables needed to answer a question from a pixel-compressed context, and then reasons over those at native resolution. On long documents, our method saves 41% of total tokens and gains 7 accuracy points over single-step QA with native resolution tables. It also uses 15% fewer tokens than the most efficient single-step compressed configuration, with no accuracy loss.

</details>


### [281] [From Atomic to Agentic: Towards Interpretable Evaluation of LLMs' Agentic Mathematical Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26950)
*Jiayi Kuang,Yinghui Li,Yunze Song,Keyu Chen,Zhifeng Shen,Yangning Li,Yidong Wang,Di Yin,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun,Kai Jin,Ying Shen,Liang Lin,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向大语言模型（LLM）智能体数学推理能力的过程级基准。该基准不同于仅评测最终答案的传统数学基准，而是将智能体解题行为与可复用的数学原子能力分类体系对齐，设计了涵盖文本与多模态场景的规划、行动与反馈三类任务，并通过自动化流程合成高质量轨迹和细粒度标注。实验表明，端到端准确率相近的模型可能具有明显不同的智能体能力画像，说明过程级评测对揭示模型真实潜力至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数数学基准只评测最终答案，属于结果导向型评测，难以诊断模型在解题过程中的失败环节或逻辑严谨性，因此无法有效指导LLM从端到端数学推理向健壮智能体转型。

Method: 构建过程级基准框架：将解题智能体行为与结构化的可复用数学原子能力分类体系对齐；设计涵盖规划、行动、反馈三类任务的评估套件，覆盖文本与多模态上下文；通过受控的LLM改写实现自动化流水线，用于合成高质量解题轨迹并生成细粒度标注。

Result: 实验发现，端到端准确率相近的模型可以表现出显著不同的智能体能力画像，证明仅依赖结果准确率无法区分模型在过程层面（如规划、行动、反馈）的真实能力差异。

Conclusion: 过程级评测对于解读LLM的真实潜力至关重要，是指导下一代数学智能体开发的关键工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are evolving from performing end-to-end mathematical reasoning to integrating agentic intelligence. However, most existing math benchmarks evaluate only final answers. This outcome-oriented evaluation provides limited diagnostic value for identifying process-level failures or rigorous logic, failing to guide the transformation of LLMs into robust agents. To bridge this gap, we present a process-level benchmark designed to evaluate the inherent agentic mathematical reasoning abilities of LLMs. Our framework aligns problem-solving agentic behaviors with a structured taxonomy of reusable mathematical atomic capabilities. We design a comprehensive suite of planning, action, and feedback tasks across both textual and multimodal contexts, supported by an automated pipeline that synthesizes high-quality trajectories and produces fine-grained annotations via controlled LLM rewriting. Experiments reveal that models with similar end-to-end accuracy can exhibit markedly different agentic capability profiles. This demonstrates that process-level evaluation is crucial for interpreting the true potential of LLMs and guiding the development of next-generation mathematical agents.

</details>


### [282] [GraphMemix: Query-Aware Evidence Forests for Long-Term Multimodal Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26983)
*Geng Li,Yuhao Wang,Dong Li,Jianye Hao,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GraphMemix，一种用于多模态智能体长期记忆组织的组合优化图记忆框架。它将记忆组织建模为查询感知的证据森林构建问题，通过候选图构建、证据效用与激活成本解耦、以及森林优化三个组件，在最大证据预算下联合选择森林格式的记忆上下文及其可靠关系结构，从而避免昂贵的离线摘要和朴素嵌入相似度匹配带来的不完整与冗余上下文问题。在四个长期多模态记忆基准上，该方法在不同基础模型下均取得显著改进，并建立了准确率与生命周期成本之间新的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆组织方法存在两类缺陷：一类依赖与查询无关的昂贵离线摘要，生命周期成本高；另一类依赖朴素的嵌入相似度匹配，会引入不完整和冗余的上下文。两者均难以在准确率与生命周期成本之间取得良好平衡，因此需要一种查询感知、可组织可靠关系结构的记忆框架。

Method: GraphMemix 将记忆组织形式化为查询感知的证据森林构建问题，包含三个关键组件：(1) 候选图构建——通过模式关系与语义关系扩展多视角种子记忆，获取查询感知的原始上下文；(2) 证据效用与激活成本——将直接记忆支持与锚定条件的关系验证解耦，抑制冗余或冲突信息；(3) 森林优化——在最大证据预算下联合选择森林格式的记忆上下文及其可靠关系结构。整体将记忆组织为查询相关的子图，避免大规模生命周期成本并恢复低相似度的互补证据。

Result: 在四个长期多模态记忆基准上的实验表明，GraphMemix 在不同基础模型下均取得显著性能提升，并在准确率与生命周期成本之间确立了新的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: GraphMemix 通过将记忆组织建模为图上的组合优化问题，有效兼顾了查询感知的上下文获取、冗余抑制与结构可靠性，在提升多模态智能体长期记忆准确性的同时显著降低生命周期成本，为长期记忆管理提供了一种更优的帕累托平衡方案。

Abstract: Organizing long-term memory for multimodal agents remains challenging because existing methods either suffer from expensive question-agnostic offline summaries or naive embedding similarity matching that introduces incomplete and redundant context. To address these issues, we propose GraphMemix, a combinatorial-optimization graph memory framework that models memory organization as query-aware evidence-forest construction. Specifically, our method consists of three key components:(1) candidate graph construction, which expands multi-view seed memories through schema and semantic relations to acquire query-aware original context; (2) evidence utility and activation costs, which decouples direct memory support from anchor-conditioned relation verification to suppress redundant or conflicting information; and (3) forest optimization, which jointly selects a forest-format memory context under a maximum evidence budget and its reliable relational structure. By organizing memory into a query-relevant subgraph, the method avoids substantial lifecycle cost and recovers low-similarity complementary evidence. Experimental results across four long-term multimodal memory benchmarks demonstrate significant improvements with different foundation models and establish a new Pareto frontier between accuracy and lifecycle cost.

</details>


### [283] [DSA: Evidence-Aware LLM-Agent Orchestration for Multi-Market Stock Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26990)
*Linsen Zhu,Yi Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 DSA（证据感知型编排框架），利用 LLM 智能体进行多市场股票研究。该框架将工作流组织为证据获取、结构化上下文构建、模型路由分析、可选的职能角色与策略技能推理、以及带诊断的报告生成；提供默认报告画像与可选的智能体画像，共享证据与路由服务但各自拥有输出验证与风险防护。参考实现覆盖六个区域市场路径、十五个内置策略技能、托管与本地模型路由及多种交付面。在冻结快照下，1,457 项可移植离线后端契约测试全部通过，其中 596 项被回溯映射到六个契约族；作者明确说明这仅证明软件实现符合契约，而非报告质量、预测准确度或投资收益更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽能概括财务信息，但可运营的股票研究系统必须先组装异构证据、揭示不可用数据与模型能力边界，并控制生成的看法如何影响最终报告——即需要一种能协调证据、模型与风险控制的系统性框架，而非单一的"摘要式"LLM 应用。

Method: DSA 将工作流划分为五个阶段：证据获取、结构化上下文构建、模型路由分析、可选的角色/策略技能推理、以及基于筛选上下文与诊断的报告生成。系统支持默认报告画像与智能体画像；智能体画像中，核心角色输出由角色专用解析器处理，策略技能意见先经"信号合格性"筛选再合成，分歧被显式传递给决策智能体，后接保守风险覆盖。参考实现包含六个区域市场路径、十五个策略技能、托管/本地模型双路由及多种执行交付面，并通过契约测试验证实现一致性。

Result: 在冻结的软件快照下，1,457 项可移植离线后端契约测试全部通过；其中 596 个用例被追溯映射到与所报道 LLM 智能体架构相关的六个契约族。作者明确限定该证据的意义：它确立的是所测试软件契约的实现符合度，而非更优的报告质量、预测准确性或投资回报。

Conclusion: DSA 提供了将 LLM 智能体落地到股票研究系统的工程化编排方案，强调证据感知、契约化实现与风险控制（如分歧显式传递与保守覆盖）的重要性；其验证以软件契约一致性为边界，不宣称金融表现上的优越性。

Abstract: Large language models can summarize financial information, but an operational stock-research system must first assemble heterogeneous evidence, expose unavailable data and model capabilities, and control how generated opinions affect a final report. We present DSA, an evidence-aware orchestration framework for multi-market stock research with large language model (LLM) agents. DSA organizes the workflow into evidence acquisition, structured context construction, model-routed analysis, optional role and Strategy Skill reasoning, and report generation with selected context and diagnostics. A default report profile and an optional agentic profile share evidence and model-routing services but use profile-specific output validation and risk safeguards. In the agentic profile, core role outputs are processed by role-specific parsers, whereas Strategy Skill opinions undergo an additional signal-eligibility partition before synthesis; disagreement is supplied explicitly to the decision agent, followed by a conservative risk override. The reference implementation includes six regional market paths, fifteen bundled Strategy Skills, hosted and local model routes, and multiple execution and delivery surfaces. At a frozen software snapshot, a selected manifest of 1,457 portable offline backend contract tests passed; 596 cases were retrospectively mapped to six contract families central to the reported LLM-agent architecture. This evidence establishes implementation conformance for the tested software contracts, not superior report quality, forecasting accuracy, or investment returns.

</details>


### [284] [ASIL: Replacing Screenshot-and-Click with Structured State and Semantic Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26991)
*Rui Xie,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASIL（Agent-Software Interaction Layer，智能体-软件交互层），一种面向软件操作智能体的“智能体原生”接口。与截图-点击范式相比，ASIL通过结构化的JSON观测和可执行代码的语义动作暴露软件能力，并利用每个应用最深的可行访问路径实现。作者在15个应用、300个单应用任务和80个多应用任务上进行了验证：ASIL在闭源模型上成功率超过80%，且每任务动作数少于5个；在50步截图预算下，同一任务集上的截图-点击控制严格成功率仅为6.6%和26.6%；ASIL在匹配任务上超越LibreOffice UNO API 28-38个百分点，与draw.io的MCP内容契约相当；小规模SFT可将Qwen3.5-2B从58.0提升至72.1、Qwen3.5-9B从66.6提升至80.4，受限资源的在线强化学习可进一步提升至74.4和82.2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强大的代码智能体虽能执行脚本、调用工具和管理文件，但许多重要应用仍主要通过图形用户界面访问。作者论证截图-点击对软件操作智能体是一种低效接口：截图状态不完整，GUI动作脆弱、语义弱，且难以匹配长时程规划。因此需要一种更适合智能体的软件交互接口。

Method: 提出ASIL，该接口通过结构化JSON观测和代码可执行的语义动作暴露软件能力，并为每个应用实现“最深处可行访问路径”（deepest feasible access path）。作者在15个应用上实例化ASIL，构建了300个单应用任务和80个多应用任务的基准；对比了截图-点击控制、应用原生接口（LibreOffice UNO API、draw.io MCP），并测试了小规模SFT和受限资源的在线RL训练。

Result: ASIL在闭源模型下成功率超过80%，每任务平均动作少于5个。在修复的运行时和50步截图预算下，同类任务的截图-点击控制严格成功率为6.6%（多应用）和26.6%（单应用）；在较易的OSWorld可比区间上分别升至15.0%和53.3%。在匹配任务上，ASIL超过LibreOffice UNO API 28-38个严格成功率点，但与draw.io的MCP内容契约持平。小规模SFT将Qwen3.5-2B从58.0提升至72.1、Qwen3.5-9B从66.6提升至80.4，受限资源在线RL进一步提升至74.4和82.2。

Conclusion: 结构化模态（而非截图）更适合作为软件操作智能体的交互接口：ASIL在效率（动作数少）、成功率与可训练性上均显著优于截图-点击范式，并在多数情况下可与甚至超越应用原生API/MCP接口；结构化模态对训练友好，小规模SFT与资源受限的在线RL即可带来显著收益。

Abstract: Powerful code agents can execute scripts, call tools, and manage files, yet many important applications remain accessible primarily through graphical user interfaces. We argue that screenshot-and-click is an inefficient interface for software-operating agents: screenshots are state-incomplete, and GUI actions are brittle, semantically weak, and poorly matched to long-horizon planning. We introduce ASIL (Agent-Software Interaction Layer), an agent-native interface that exposes software through structured JSON observations and code-executable semantic actions, realized through the deepest feasible access path for each application. We instantiate ASIL across 15 applications and a benchmark of 300 single-application and 80 multi-application tasks. ASIL reaches above 80 with closed models while executing fewer than five actions per task. Under a repaired runtime and a 50-step screenshot budget, the same tasks yield 6.6 and 26.6 strict success under screenshot-and-click control, rising to 15.0 and 53.3 on an easier OSWorld-comparable band. Against application-native interfaces on matched tasks, ASIL exceeds LibreOffice's UNO API by 28-38 strict points but only matches draw.io's MCP content contract. The structured modality also suits training: small-scale SFT raises Qwen3.5-2B from 58.0 to 72.1 and Qwen3.5-9B from 66.6 to 80.4, and resource-limited on-policy RL further raises them to 74.4 and 82.2.

</details>


### [285] [A Multi-Modal AI Framework for Real-Time Queue Prediction, Management and Optimisation in Intelligent Border Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27010)
*Varvara Mama,Eleni Veroni,Nikolaos Kapsalis,Christos D. Nikolopoulos,Anargyros T. Baklezos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态人工智能（AI）的高效边境控制管理流程，通过集成LSTM网络进行实时队列预测、结合模型预测控制（MPC）与调度优化生成控制策略，从而在动态交通条件下实现自适应边境流量管理。在合成数据上的评估显示，该方法相比ARIMA和基于规则的方法，队列预测误差降低最多35%，平均等待时间降低30%，平均吞吐量提升近20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运营队列管理系统大多依赖静态数据进行操作，难以应对动态交通条件和不确定性，无法实现最优性能。因此需要一种考虑实时交通状况、具备自适应能力的边境控制管理方案。

Method: 提出一个多模态AI框架：(1) 集成异构数据源，通过统一表示呈现，并使用长短期记忆网络（LSTM）进行队列预测；(2) 利用模型预测控制（MPC）和调度优化推导可执行的管控策略；(3) 将控制策略呈现给边境管制人员。采用模拟真实交通的合成数据进行评估。

Result: 与ARIMA和基于规则的方法相比，所提方法使队列预测误差降低达35%，平均等待时间降低30%，平均吞吐量提高约20%。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了将AI架构与优化技术相结合用于主动、自适应边境交通管理的有效性与高效性。

Abstract: In the present work an efficient border control management procedure is proposed. Compared to operational queue management systems, whose operations are based on mostly static data, the proposed work takes into account dynamic traffic conditions, thus enabling optimal performance, even in cases of uncertainty. To this end, we are proposing a multi-modal Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework, tailored to th needs of border control systems, which enables real-time queue prediction, management, and resource optimization. The novel proposed approach integrates heterogeneous data sources and presents them through a unified representation by employing Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks for queue forecasting. Furthermore, it leverages Model Predictive Control (MPC) and scheduling optimization to derive actionable control policies, which in turn can be presented to border control officers. The proposed work has been evaluated using synthetic data simulating realistic traffic. The evaluation results demonstrate that the proposed method reduces queue prediction error by up to 35% and average waiting time by 30%. Accordingly, the average throughput increases by nearly 20%, compared to ARIMA and rule-based methods. The abovementioned results show the effectiveness and efficiency of combining AI architectures with optimization techniques for proactive and adaptive border traffic management.

</details>


### [286] [Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27044)
*Wei-Yao Wang,Kazuya Tateishi,Shuyang Cui,Christian Simon,Takashi Shibuya,Shusuke Takahashi,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniUE（Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder），这是首个支持全模态（文本、视频、音频）统一嵌入空间与全交互式查询的通用嵌入器。它利用专用可学习令牌的中间层表示构建统一嵌入空间，并通过视觉/音频分割器与 omni-LLM 融合，支持用户以文本、视觉感兴趣区域、音频片段等形式进行条件化任意到任意嵌入。作者还提出 OmniCHOIR 基准用于评估全交互组合式音频检索，OmniUE 在多模态基准上全面超越 SOTA（文本交互视频 +10.5%、音频 +1.1%、视觉交互 +83.7%、OmniCHOIR +24.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态表示学习正从传统的双塔架构转向基于大语言模型（LLM）的嵌入器，因为后者具有强大的指令遵循能力。然而现有方法主要聚焦于语言和图像两种模态，且用户条件化交互也以这两种模态为主，缺乏对视频、音频以及任意组合交互方式的支持。作者旨在推进全模态表示学习与全交互式查询的统一，为构建真正的通用嵌入器铺路。

Method: OmniUE 的方法包括两部分：（1）通过专用可学习令牌的中间层表示，在文本、视频、音频之间学习统一嵌入空间；（2）利用视觉与音频分割器处理多样的用户交互输入（文本、视觉感兴趣区域、音频片段），将其与 omni-LLM 集成，通过上下文聚合产生用户条件化的任意到任意（any-to-any）嵌入。此外，作者提出 OmniCHOIR 基准，专门评测基于给定文本、视频、音频以及单模态或多模态交互提示的全交互组合式音频检索能力。

Result: OmniUE 在多种模态基准上持续超越 SOTA 基线：MMEB-v2-video（文本交互视频基准）平均提升 10.5%，MAEB（音频任务）提升 1.1%，SCaR（视觉交互基准）提升 83.7%，自建全交互基准 OmniCHOIR 提升 24.1%。

Conclusion: 作者认为，将全模态表示学习与全交互式查询共同推进，是实现通用嵌入器的关键路径。OmniUE 验证了该方向的可行性，并展示了显著的性能优势。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning has been shifting from traditional two-tower architectures to large language model (LLM)-based embedders due to their strong instruction-following capabilities. Despite this progress, existing approaches primarily focus on language and image modalities, which also remain the dominant modalities for user-conditioned interactions in current embedders. In this paper, we propose the first Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder (OmniUE), which not only learns a unified embedding space across text, video, and audio by leveraging intermediate-layer representations from dedicated learnable tokens, but also supports omni-interactive querying, enabling users to provide inputs in the form of text, visual regions of interest, and audio spans. Within OmniUE, visual and audio segmenters process diverse user interactions and integrate them with an omni-LLM to produce user-conditioned any-to-any embeddings via context aggregation. To evaluate OmniUE's omni-interactive capabilities, we introduce OmniCHOIR, benchmarking models for omni-interactive compositional audio retrieval based on the given text, video, and audio as well as unimodal or multimodal interaction prompts. OmniUE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art baselines across diverse modalities, with average improvements of 10.5% on textual-interactive video benchmarks (MMEB-v2-video), 1.1% on audio tasks (MAEB), 83.7% on visual-interactive benchmarks (SCaR), and 24.1% on our omni-interactive OmniCHOIR benchmark. We believe that jointly advancing omni-modal representation learning and omni-interactive querying paves the way toward universal embedders.

</details>


### [287] [A Contract-Centered Architecture for Scalable and Manageable Agentic Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27086)
*Yaxiao Liu,Pengbo Liu,Yiwen Liu,Yihua Guan,Zhenghe Hou,Jiaxing Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对企业级AI部署中的跨团队协调问题，提出一组共享组织契约：四个责任对象（Skill、Harness、Scaffold及CIO治理的数据基座），并围绕可证伪假设P1（成本感知的能力-容量可分离性）设计了集群-周期随机交叉实验及四状态判定协议。论文当前仅贡献架构、数据基座与测量协议，尚无完成的实现、实验、数据集或实测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署本质上是业务单元、应用与AI团队、测试、平台工程、基础设施、安全、运维和数据治理等多方之间的协调问题。现有用例基准仅衡量单个智能体能否完成单任务，无法回答能力、模型、运行时机制、容量与企业数据应如何共同地拥有、变更、准入和举证。作者因此希望建立一套可作为组织间共享契约的责任对象与可证伪的测量框架，以弥合这一缺口。

Method: 提出四个责任对象：Skill（可复用、可版本化的能力与工作流资产）、Harness（运行时编译器与治理器）、Scaffold（执行/控制边界与NFR责任主体），以及位于技术栈外部、在独立CIO治理语义与遥测下的数据基座；运行时核心为A=<S,H,X>。核心贡献是可证伪假设P1（成本感知的能力-容量可分离性）：在声明的运行区域内，能力变更在预先登记的等价裕度内保持容量-响应交互，兼容的Scaffold容量变更在非劣效裕度内保持能力语义，所需控制落在声明的执行预算内。六个设计条件转化为可测量的义务，其覆盖度、违规、不确定性、成本与排除项决定P1是否可判定。实验采用集群-周期随机交叉设计（平衡顺序、重置/洗脱、重复种子与故障模式、集群感知的不确定性），给出四种判定状态：支持、证伪、条件工程、尚无定论。

Result: 论文报告尚无完成的实现、实验、数据集或测量结果，因此未提供经验性结果或实证验证。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于三个方面：契约约束的运行时架构、保持源语义的数据基座，以及可证伪的测量协议。其将六个设计条件转为可测量义务，并以明确的实验协议支持对P1的可判定性与四状态裁决，为后续实证工作提供了理论框架与操作化路径。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployment is a coordination problem across business units, application and AI teams, testing, platform engineering, infrastructure, security, operations, and data governance. Use-case benchmarks show whether one agent completes one task, but not how changing capabilities, models, runtime mechanisms, capacity, and enterprise data should be owned, changed, admitted, or evidenced together.
  We present four responsibility objects as shared organizational contracts: Skill (reusable, versioned capability and workflow asset), Harness (runtime compiler and governor), Scaffold (execution/control boundary and NFR owner), and a stack-external data substrate under independent CIO-governed semantics and telemetry. The runtime core is A = <S, H, X>, with the data substrate outside that stack.
  The central contribution is one bounded, falsifiable hypothesis, P1 (cost-aware capability-capacity separability): within a declared operating region, changing activated capability preserves the capacity-response interaction within a preregistered equivalence margin, while changing compatible Scaffold capacity preserves capability semantics up to a non-inferiority margin, and the required controls stay within a declared enforcement budget. Six design conditions become measured obligations whose coverage, violations, uncertainty, cost, and exclusions determine whether P1 is decidable.
  We propose a cluster-period randomized crossover experiment (balanced order, reset/washout, repeated seeds and failure regimes, cluster-aware uncertainty) with a four-state verdict: supported, falsified, conditional-engineering, or inconclusive. This paper contributes a contract-bounded runtime architecture, a source-preserving data substrate, and a falsifiable measurement protocol. It reports no completed implementation, experiment, dataset, or measured result.

</details>


### [288] [pro-team at LLMs4OL 2026 Tasks Flagship and Reuse: Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Vocabulary-Constrained Filtering for Ontology Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27101)
*Shivam Mishra,Dhannu Ram Meena,Muneendra Ojha,Krishna Pratap Singh,Kuldeep Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种离线检索增强的小样本提示（retrieval-augmented few-shot prompting）流水线，用于参加 LLMs4OL 2026 挑战赛的端到端本体学习（Task A）与本体扩展复用（Task B）两个任务。系统基于 Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct，使用 all-MiniLM-L6-v2 检索相似演示样本（Task A 取 top-5，Task B 取 top-2），并采用左截断的上下文窗口策略以保留任务指令。Task B 中还结合基于封闭词表的确定性过滤与去重。实验结果表明 Task B 的语义图相似度（Semantic Graph Similarity）达 0.8692、术语类型标注 F1 为 0.9200、分类发现 F1 为 0.8540；Task A 的语义图相似度为 0.7416。但系统未抽取任何非分类（associative/非taxonomy）关系，暴露了封闭且面向分类的关系词表带来的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
