An agent-based modeling framework coupled with the Simulation of Urban MObility (SUMO) simulator is used to evaluate Deep Q-Network (DQN), Double Deep Q-Network (DDQN), and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). Results show that PPO outperforms DQN and DDQN, achieving a joining success rate of approximately 98 % and a collision rate below 1 %, largely due to risk-related penalties incorporated into the reward function. However, this improved performance requires more decision steps to complete the maneuver, revealing a trade-off between safety, joining effectiveness, and decision efficiency. An external safety controller effectively prevents collisions, although its interventions may reduce joining efficiency. The results highlight the importance of jointly considering safety and efficiency when designing RL-based controllers for CAV platoon joining in mixed traffic.

</details>


### [353] [When Is the Sharp Covariance Envelope Tight? Feature-Only Geometry for Volume-Sampled Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26877)
*Kihun Rhee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在“普通索引固定尺寸体积抽样 + 所选非加权最小二乘”框架下，对任意满秩固定数据池、响应与合法预算 d ≤ s ≤ m，建立了中心化系数协方差的全 Gram 白化 Loewner 包络，其系数在满秩类上全局精确；并在特征边际 ν_A 下给出精确的固定设计谱相变（严格/紧）及其残差相容刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Derezinski 与 Warmuth 的先期工作确立了全尺寸抽样恒等式、selected-OLS 无偏性与逆矩结果，但其精确的任意固定响应损失与预测协方差公式仅位于秩-尺寸端点 s=d 处。本文旨在填补一般预算 d < s ≤ m 的空缺，刻画中心化系数协方差（系数估计围绕其自身期望的散布，而非围绕真参数）的分析界及其谱特性，并量化固定数据池上的可达到性。

Method: 方法包括：以全 Gram 白化后的中心化系数协方差为对象构造 Loewner 包络并证明其在满秩类上全局精确；引入仅依赖特征的边际 ν_A（无环、严格内部预算、正损失条件下）作为谱相判据；通过残差增广的测度变换获得响应感知机制与单侧定量松弛界；用支撑饱和论证证明达到（attainment）方向，用临界等杠杆几何解释边界，并借助可靠的（sound）下证书做出保守的同原始基（same-primitive）基数决策。

Result: 对每个满秩固定池、响应与预算 d ≤ s ≤ m，得到全局精确的 Loewner 包络；全局精确性本身不决定在具体数据池上的可达到性；在正损失、严格内部预算且无 coloop 条件下，ν_A>0 当且仅当归一化谱包络对每个相容残差严格，ν_A=0 当且仅当存在相容残差在谱上紧，且该零边际残差在所有严格内部预算下保持紧；冻结特征示例表明证书非空并量化授权缩减在固定池上的代价。

Conclusion: 本文结论限定于条件中心化、全 Gram 白化的系数协方差，而非总体泛化风险；它统一了此前仅有的秩-尺寸端点结果，把体积抽样的协方差上界推广到全部合法预算，并通过谱相变刻画了边界严格性与可达到性的完整机制，为固定池上的保守决策提供可计算证书。

Abstract: Prior analyses by Derezinski and Warmuth established all-size sampling identities, selected-OLS unbiasedness, and inverse moments for ordinary volume sampling, while their exact arbitrary-fixed-response loss and prediction-covariance formulas are at the rank-size endpoint s=d. We establish a Loewner envelope for centered coefficient covariance for every full-rank fixed pool, response, and legal budget d <= s <= m under ordinary indexed fixed-size volume sampling followed by selected unweighted least squares; its coefficient is globally sharp over the full-rank class. Global sharpness does not determine attainability on the pool in hand. Under positive loss, strict-interior budgets, and no coloops, a feature-only margin nu_A gives the exact fixed-design spectral phase: nu_A > 0 if and only if the normalized spectral envelope is strict for every compatible residual, whereas nu_A = 0 if and only if some compatible residual is spectrally tight; the same zero-margin residual is tight at every strict-interior budget. A residual-augmented change of measure supplies the response-aware mechanism and a one-sided quantitative slack bound, while support saturation proves the attainment direction. Critical equal-leverage geometry interprets the boundary, and sound lower certificates yield conservative same-primitive cardinality decisions. Frozen-feature examples show that the certificate is nonvacuous and measure the fixed-pool cost of its authorized reduction. The claims concern conditional centered, full-Gram-whitened coefficient covariance, not population generalization.

</details>


### [354] [A Layer Importance Metric for Quantization Accounting for the Speed-Quality Trade-off in Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26926)
*Artem Safronov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需实际执行的复合优先级指标，用于指导小型语言模型（sLLM）的量化加速：该指标结合信息保留度（基于归一化SQNR系数）与吞吐增益（基于roofline延迟建模），在Gemma 3 1B上识别出前馈网络（FFN）和嵌入矩阵为最优量化目标，并对其他架构验证了约4%的加速预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型部署在内存和算力受限的设备上，自回归推理受内存带宽限制，而均匀量化常对sLLM有害——因为其架构冗余有限，仅少数层对低精度不敏感。因此需要一种可预测的、能权衡速度与质量的量化优先级选择方法，避免依赖昂贵的近似推理或穷举搜索。

Method: 提出复合度量，由两个正交准则组成：(1) 信息保留度，以归一化SQNR为基础系数衡量；(2) 吞吐增益，基于roofline模型进行延迟分析，无需实际执行。对Gemma 3 1B进行profiling，找出FFN块与嵌入矩阵为最合适的加速目标；对每个候选对象分别估计归一化质量分数（基于模拟量化）与归一化速度分数（基于roofline建模），二者合成复合优先级系数，可调节速度与质量的权衡。该度量具有通用性，可用于单个块、投影子层或整个Transformer层。

Result: 在多个模型架构上的评估显示，加速预测误差约为4%。与传统进化搜索、专用加速器或基于Shapley值（需昂贵近似推理）的方法相比，本方法通常将更多资源分配给最具表现力的层。

Conclusion: 该分析方法使sLLM量化成为可预测的工程任务，为受限设备上的模型压缩提供了一种通用、可调且高效的优先级量化策略。

Abstract: Small language models (sLLMs) are nowadays hosted on devices with limited memory and computational budget. In an autoregressive setup, inference is memory-bandwidth bound: uniform quantization is often detrimental to such models, since their architecture has limited redundancies and only a few layers are not very sensitive to lower precision. We propose a composite metric that combines two orthogonal criteria: information retention (measured in terms of a normalized SQNR-based coefficient) and throughput gains (modeled using a roofline-based latency analysis). By profiling Gemma 3 1B, we find that Feed-Forward Network blocks and the embedding matrix are the most promising targets for acceleration. For each candidate, we estimate a normalized quality score based on simulated quantization and a normalized speed score based on roofline modeling with no actual execution needed. We combine the two scores in a composite priority coefficient, allowing us to tune the trade-off between speed and quality as needed. Our metric is general and can be used to prioritize individual blocks, their projection sublayers, or transformer layers as a whole. We evaluate our approach on several model architectures, showing that our estimates have at around 4% prediction error for the accelerated speedup. We find that our method generally allocates more resources to the most expressive layers compared to evolutionary search, specialized accelerators, or Shapley-value-based approaches that require expensive approximate inference. Our analytical approach makes sLLM quantization a predictable engineering task.

</details>


### [355] [Scaling Model-Generated Distillation Data Can Make Latent Teacher Traits More Recoverable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26958)
*Zhichen Dong,Zhixuan Liu,Yuyu Fan,Xiangtian Li,Shuyang Zhang,Chao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现：扩大模型生成式蒸馏数据不仅提升蒸馏质量，还会放大教师模型中隐藏的微妙“特质”信号——即使数据完全离题、不提及该特质，学生模型也能在更大数据规模下更清晰地学到并泛化该特质。这一效应在多种模型、特质类型、多特质及跨模型迁移中均存在，表明扩展生成数据时需要配合“特质感知”的筛选与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究普遍将扩大模型生成数据的规模视为纯粹利好：更多样本应提升覆盖度、降低噪声、训练出更强的学生模型。作者质疑这一假设，提出数据规模可能还有第二个效应——放大教师模型中细微且不易察觉的特质信号，即便数据看似离题或无害。他们希望在受控实验下揭示并量化这种“超感知式”（subliminal）的隐性特征迁移。

Method: 采用受“阈下学习”启发的受控设置：诱导教师模型表达某个目标特质，使其生成受限的离题数据（如仅含数字的补全）；将不同数量的独立离题数据作为训练集训练学生模型，并在独立领域进行评估；通过匹配的无特质对照组隔离出目标特异的迁移效应。同时分析学生模型学到的LoRA更新，并与跨模型家族、跨特质类型、多特质设定、跨模型迁移等条件相互验证。

Result: 核心发现是：更大的独立数据集会使教师诱导出的特质在学生后续行为中更清晰。其他看似合理的特质也可能随规模增强，但目标特质通常增长更快。当小规模学生已偏向目标特质时，扩规模主要放大该行为；当小规模学生偏向相关或显著替代特质时，更多数据可把行为转向目标特质。LoRA更新分析呈现平行趋势，且效应在模型家族、特质类型、多特质及跨模型迁移中均可复现。

Conclusion: 扩展生成式蒸馏数据应当配合特质感知的筛选与评估机制，即使数据看起来离题或良性，也可能在规模扩大时放大教师模型隐蔽的特质偏好，从而影响学生模型的行为。这对大规模蒸馏数据的构建与评测提出了新的规范要求。

Abstract: Scaling model-generated data is usually viewed as improving distillation: more examples should increase coverage, reduce noise, and produce stronger students. We show a second effect: larger datasets can make subtle teacher-specific signals easier to detect in the trained student, even when examples are off-task and never mention the trait. In a controlled setup inspired by subliminal learning, a teacher induced to express a target trait generates restricted off-task data, such as number-only completions. Students trained on different amounts of independent off-task data are evaluated in a separate domain, with matched no-trait controls isolating target-specific transfer. Our main finding is that larger independent datasets make the teacher's induced trait stand out more clearly in the student's later behavior. Other plausible traits may also strengthen with scale, but the target usually grows more. When the small-scale student already favors the target, scaling mainly amplifies that behavior; when it favors a related or salient alternative, more data can shift behavior toward the intended trait. Analyses of learned LoRA updates show a parallel trend. These effects appear across model families, trait types, multi-trait settings, and cross-model transfer. Our results suggest that scaling generated distillation data should be paired with trait-aware curation and evaluation, even when the data appears off-task or benign.

</details>


### [356] [Gromov-Monge Flow Matching for Equivariant Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26961)
*Moritz Piening,Christian Wald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究流匹配生成模型中的图对称性：在比较节点重排下的图对时，自然采用图商空间的Gromov–Monge距离作为Wasserstein几何。理论上证明商耦合可无损提升为对齐代表，且对称化可得到等变流匹配最优解；实践中用Gromov–Wasserstein松弛和节点对齐下界构造小批量耦合，仅改变训练耦合即可与标准置换等变架构兼容，显著提升小积分预算下的样本质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图对节点置换具有不变性，生成模型中常用置换等变架构；但流匹配中对称性还体现在源-目标耦合上。当图对在节点重标签意义下比较时，自然的度量几何是图商空间，其欧氏商度量恰好等于Gromov–Monge距离。作者希望从理论到实践发展这一视角，使耦合本身具备结构感知能力。

Method: 理论层面：(1) 证明商空间中的耦合可以无额外代价提升为对齐后的代表样本；(2) 证明对称化可得到等变流匹配最小化器，包括分类端点预测情形。实践层面：精确Gromov–Monge对齐不可行，因此利用高效的Gromov–Wasserstein类松弛以及节点内对齐的下界构造小批量耦合，可选地与图间外层分配结合。该流程只改变训练耦合，并兼容标准置换等变架构。

Result: 在连续图生成与分类分子生成任务上，结构感知耦合在小积分预算下显著提升样本质量；扩大规模的分子模型在常规多步采样下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 耦合中的对称性（而非仅架构中的等变性）对图生成质量至关重要；Gromov–Monge/Wasserstein类结构感知耦合是一种即插即用的训练策略，成本低、兼容性强，并在低步数采样场景下增益尤为明显。

Abstract: Graphs are invariant under node permutations, motivating the use of permutation-equivariant architectures in generative models. In flow matching, however, symmetry may also enter the source--target coupling: once graph pairs are compared up to node relabeling, the natural Wasserstein geometry is that of the graph quotient space. The Euclidean quotient metric of this space coincides with the Gromov--Monge distance, obtained by optimally relabeling the nodes. We develop this perspective theoretically, showing that quotient couplings can be lifted to aligned representatives without additional cost and that symmetrization yields equivariant flow-matching minimizers, including for categorical endpoint prediction. In practice, exact Gromov--Monge alignment is intractable, so we construct minibatch couplings using efficient Gromov--Wasserstein-type relaxations and lower bounds for the inner node alignment, optionally combined with an outer assignment between graphs. The resulting procedure changes only the training coupling and is compatible with standard permutation-equivariant architectures. Across continuous graph and categorical molecular generation, these structure-aware couplings substantially improve sample quality at small integration budgets, while our scaled-up molecular models remain competitive under conventional many-step sampling.

</details>


### [357] [Packora: Systematic Design for Generative Molecular Crystal Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26962)
*Nayoung Kim,Kiyoung Seong,Sungsoo Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Packora 是一个基于流的生成模型，用于分子晶体结构预测（CSP），可从分子图联合预测原子坐标与晶格。它支持多组分与有机金属晶体，且能在单一模型中按任意子集条件化分子构象、立体化学标签和空间群信息。在生成与排序基准上均优于基线，并在六个生成基准上取得最佳匹配预算覆盖率，同时获得更高的实验形态恢复率、更低的实验形态排名和更快的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子晶体结构预测在制药、农化和有机电子领域至关重要，因为分子构象与堆积方式的细微差异会强烈影响材料性质。构建一个能高效、准确地预测晶体结构的生成模型具有重要的应用价值。

Method: 提出 Packora：一种基于流的生成模型，从分子图联合预测原子坐标与晶格。模型支持多组分及有机金属晶体，并可在单一模型内对分子构象子集、立体化学标签和空间群信息进行条件化。受 CCDC CSP 盲测启发，将生成与排序分开评估：生成用于隔离生成器质量，排序用于衡量在统一弛豫与排序流程下的端到端性能。同时系统研究了架构、训练、条件化、推理与扩展，确定了基于可缓存成对推理、训练目标与数值求解器选择、条件化 dropout、单/双表示平衡扩展的有效设计。

Result: Packora 在结构生成与排序基准上均超过基线：在全部六个生成基准上取得最佳匹配预算覆盖率，并实现更高的实验形态恢复率、更低的实验形态排名以及更快的排序收敛。

Conclusion: 通过将生成与排序分离评估，并系统优化架构、训练与条件化设计，Packora 为分子晶体结构预测提供了高效且可扩展的生成式解决方案，展现出比现有基线更强的生成质量与端到端性能。

Abstract: Molecular crystal structure prediction (CSP) is important in pharmaceuticals, agrochemicals, and organic electronics, where subtle differences in molecular conformation and packing can strongly affect material properties. We present Packora, a flow-based generative model for molecular CSP that jointly predicts atomic coordinates and the lattice from molecular graphs. Packora supports multi-component and organometallic crystals and can condition on any subset of molecular conformers, stereochemical labels, and space-group information within a single model. Inspired by the CCDC CSP blind test, we evaluate generation and ranking separately, using generation to isolate generator quality and ranking to measure end-to-end performance under a common relaxation and ranking pipeline. We also systematically study architecture, training, conditioning, inference, and scaling, identifying an effective design based on cacheable pairwise reasoning, training objective and numerical solver choices, conditioning dropout, and balanced scaling of pairwise and single representations. Packora outperforms the baselines on both structure generation and ranking benchmarks, achieving the best matched-budget coverage across all six generation benchmarks, as well as higher experimental-form recovery, lower experimental-form ranks, and faster convergence in ranking.

</details>


### [358] [Adversarial Training Without Input Gradients via Low-Rank Householder Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26963)
*Tiana C. Johnson,Donsub Rim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究针对小范数对抗样本的对抗训练，提出一种基于低秩Householder展开（LRHE）的线性化方法，直接从网络参数计算对抗方向，无需输入梯度迭代；并构建一个简单的对抗训练正则化器，消除了内层最大化，仅需额外前向传播，计算成本远低于标准PGD训练，在相对ℓ2预算较小的情况下匹配甚至超过PGD训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络对输入存在固有的不稳定性，导致出现范数很小（相对ℓ2范数）且难以察觉的对抗样本。现有对抗训练通常需要输入梯度迭代（如PGD）来求解内部最大化，计算代价高昂；而有些免内层搜索的方法仍需对输入求微分。作者希望探索是否可以不依赖输入微分，仅从网络参数直接获得局部几何信息，从而更高效地进行对抗训练。

Method: 作者利用低秩Householder展开（LRHE）来线性化网络的复合仿射映射，从而直接从训练好的网络参数和正向传播中已有的激活模式计算对抗方向，避免了输入梯度迭代。基于此构建了一个对抗训练正则化器：训练过程中不需要对输入做任何微分，只进行额外的正向评估，权重仍由标准反向传播更新；内层最大化被完全消除，整体只相当于每轮2.8步PGD的成本。

Result: 该正则化器每轮训练成本相当于2.8步PGD，相比MNIST上40步PGD对抗训练减少了8.7倍，且低于3步训练的成本。在相对ℓ2预算ε≤0.02时，模型匹配3步PGD对抗训练；在ε≤0.012时匹配40步训练；超出该范围后性能下降，与展开的局部性一致。

Conclusion: 主要发现是：无需输入微分也能获得局部几何信息并实现有效的对抗训练，免除了内层搜索；该方法的性能在小扰动预算下与PGD对抗训练相当，同时大幅降低计算成本，但受限于LRHE的局部近似范围。

Abstract: This work concerns adversarial training against the small-norm adversarial examples that arise from the inherent input instability of a trained deep neural network. Examples in this class are small as measured in the relative $\ell^2$-norm, and therefore lie in the neighborhood of the input on which the model acts approximately linearly, the regime in which the perturbation remains imperceptible. We first show that such examples can be computed directly from the trained network parameters, without input gradient iterations, by means of a linearization called the low-rank Householder expansion (LRHE). The expansion describes the composed affine map rather than any individual layer, and the directions it identifies are read from the activation pattern already available in the forward pass. We then propose a simple adversarial training scheme built on this construction. No differentiation with respect to the input is performed at any point: training requires only additional forward evaluations, with weight parameters updated by the standard backward pass, and the inner maximization of the usual min-max formulation is eliminated entirely. That such a regularizer exists is our main finding: the methods that dispense with the inner search all obtain their local geometry by differentiating with respect to the input, and we show this is not necessary. The regularizer costs the equivalent of $2.8$ PGD steps per epoch, an $8.7\times$ reduction relative to 40-step adversarial training on MNIST and below the cost of 3-step training. The resulting models match three-step PGD adversarial training for relative $\ell^2$ budgets $\varepsilon \le 0.02$ and 40-step training for $\varepsilon \le 0.012$, falling away beyond, consistent with the locality of the expansion.

</details>


### [359] [Graph-Based Pseudo-multimodal Contrastive Learning for 12-Lead ECG Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26964)
*Mengyu Wang,Kozo Okada,Takafumi Goto,Natsuko Jinba,Hiroki Yamaya,Kiyoshi Hibi,Tomoki Hamagami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Graph-CMMC，一个基于图的伪多模态对比学习框架，用于 12 导联心电图分析。它将 ECG 波形转换为 GADF 图像，构建同一心脏活动的互补表征以实现伪多模态学习；通过自监督方式对齐波形与 GADF 表征，并利用图关系模块建模导联间依赖并在对比学习中保持结构一致性。在多标签冠状动脉闭塞分类任务上取得了与监督学习方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有 ECG 分析方法仅关注单导联信号或独立处理各导联，且通常将 ECG 视为一维时间序列并用 CNN/RNN 建模。这类方法擅长捕捉局部波形变化，却难以刻画临床诊断中关键的导联间依赖关系与全局波形模式，因此需要一种能显式建模 12 导联间结构和互补信息的表征学习方法。

Method: 提出 Graph-CMMC 框架：(1) 将 ECG 波形转换为 Gramian Angular Difference Field (GADF) 图像，作为同一心脏活动的互补表征，构造伪多模态学习场景；(2) 利用全部 12 导联，以自监督方式对齐波形表征与 GADF 表征；(3) 引入基于图的关系模块建模导联间依赖，并在对比学习过程中强制保持跨导联的结构一致性。

Result: 在冠状动脉闭塞多标签分类任务上的实验表明，Graph-CMMC 取得了与监督学习方法相当的竞争力性能；结果验证了 GADF 作为互补表征的有效性，以及显式图建模导联间依赖对学习鲁棒 12 导联 ECG 表征的促进作用。

Conclusion: Graph-CMMC 通过伪多模态对比学习与图关系建模，弥补了传统方法在导联间依赖和全局波形建模上的不足，为 12 导联 ECG 的无监督/自监督表征学习提供了有效方案；该方法在分类性能上接近监督方法，具有实际应用潜力，后续可进一步探索更强的结构先验与下游任务适配。

Abstract: 12-lead electrocardiogram (ECG) is a standard, non-invasive examination widely used for diagnosing coronary artery disease, where clinical interpretation relies on comparing waveform patterns across multiple leads. However, most existing ECG analysis methods focus on single-lead signals or treat each lead independently, and typically process ECG signals as one-dimensional time-series data using CNNs or RNNs. While effective in modeling local waveform changes, such approaches have difficulty capturing inter-lead dependency and global waveform patterns essential for clinical diagnosis.
  To address this limitation, we propose a graph-based pseudo-multimodal contrastive learning framework called Graph-CMMC. ECG waveforms are transformed into Gramian Angular Difference Field (GADF) images to construct complementary representations of the same cardiac activity, enabling a pseudo-multimodal learning setting. Using all 12 leads, Graph-CMMC aligns waveform and GADF representations in a self-supervised manner, while a graph-based relational module is employed to model inter-lead dependency and enforce structural consistency across leads during contrastive learning.
  Experimental results on a multi-label coronary artery occlusion classification task demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves competitive performance compared to supervised learning methods. These results further suggest the effectiveness of using GADF as a complementary representation and incorporating explicit graph-based modeling of inter-lead dependency for learning robust 12-lead ECG representations.

</details>


### [360] [ClusterAttention: A training-free speedup of bidirectional attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26965)
*Kasper Nordenram,Amelie Dittmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ClusterAttention 是一种无需训练的通用双向注意力加速方法，通过快速递归聚类适应当前注意力头中键与查询的几何结构，将簇大小固定为 2 的幂以匹配 GPU 上稠密注意力的延迟。论文还推导了稀疏注意力的输出误差表达式，并提出通过簇质心补偿被排除的簇来缩小误差。在非结构化表格数据上可将 TabPFN-3 加速 2–6 倍且保持超过 99% 的稠密精度；在视频生成任务 Wan 2.1-14B 上相比 SVOO 取得更大加速（1.8x 对 1.4x）且输出更接近稠密注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有稀疏注意力方法要么依赖输入中的结构（如语言顺序、图像空间近邻），要么使用缓慢的聚类过程并在多次前向传播中摊销。本文旨在为双向注意力层提供一种既无需训练、又能在非结构化输入和单次前向传播下起作用的通用加速方案，从而覆盖现有方法的盲区。

Method: 提出快速递归聚类方法，按每个注意力头中键与查询的几何结构生成有用簇，簇大小可任意设定，并被固定为 2 的幂以实现块稀疏注意力在 GPU 上与稠密注意力相同的每查询-键交互延迟。推导出稀疏注意力输出误差的表达式，用于解释“紧密簇反而比随机簇误差更大”的反直觉现象；随后推导当被排除簇通过质心补偿时的误差，证明该误差随簇更紧密而减小，并将该质心补偿整合进方法中。

Result: 在大规模表格数据上，ClusterAttention 将 TabPFN-3 加速 2–6 倍，同时保留至少 99% 的稠密精度，是首个能成功应用于非结构化输入与单次前向传播场景的无训练方法。在视频生成 Wan 2.1-14B T2V 上，相比专为该领域设计的前沿方法 SVOO（均在无离线校准下运行），ClusterAttention 输出更接近稠密注意力且加速更大（1.8x 对比 1.4x）。

Conclusion: ClusterAttention 证明了无需任何训练或输入结构先验，即可通过适应簇几何与质心误差补偿实现高效的稀疏双向注意力，兼顾速度与精度。其理论误差分析为设计稀疏注意力提出了可操作的原则（紧密簇配合质心补偿），在表格数据与视频生成两类不同任务上均获得一致性收益。

Abstract: This paper introduces ClusterAttention, a general training-free speedup of bidirectional attention layers. Existing sparse attention methods either rely on structure in the input, such as order in language or spatial proximity in images, or use slow clustering processes amortized over several forward passes. ClusterAttention instead uses a fast recursive clustering method that adapts to the geometry of the keys and queries in each attention head to produce useful clusters. This method allows setting the size of the clusters arbitrarily. We utilize this by setting all clusters to be a fixed size that is a power of two, allowing the block-sparse attention to run at the same latency per query-key interaction as dense attention on GPUs. We also derive an expression for the output error in sparse attention, that explains the counterintuitive experimental finding that tight clusters can lead to larger errors than random clusters. We then derive the error when excluded clusters are compensated through their centroids, and show that this error shrinks with tighter clusters. We integrate this compensation into the method.
  On large-scale tabular data ClusterAttention speeds up TabPFN-3 arXiv:2605.13986 by two to six times, while retaining at least 99% of the dense accuracy. To our knowledge, it is the first training-free method that can be successfully applied in the setting of unstructured input and a single forward pass. For video generation with Wan 2.1-14B T2V arXiv:2503.20314 , ClusterAttention achieves output closer to dense attention and a larger speedup (1.8x versus 1.4x) compared to SVOO arXiv:2603.18636 , a leading method developed specifically for this domain, both run without offline calibration.

</details>


### [361] [TEMPLAR Wales: A georeferenced environmental and toponymic dataset of Welsh settlements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26970)
*Oktay Karakuş,Can Eyupoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TEMPLAR Wales 是一个地理参照的环境-地名学数据集，涵盖威尔士 3,757 条聚落记录，通过稳定标识符将可复现的聚落框架、确定性词汇筛选和聚落级环境属性相链接。它包含 24 个威尔士地名要素冻结注册表生成的 1,350 个词汇检测（分布于 1,294 个聚落），并提供河流与海岸邻近度、高程、多尺度地形、土地覆盖及邻域木本覆盖等环境属性，且配有来自独立高程产品的平行地形测量。数据以四张关系表发布，附带字段级数据字典、来源登记册和许可元数据，技术验证确认了关系完整性、确定性词汇重建、环境覆盖度、独立地形源一致性及冻结版本的复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地名承载着景观描述与组织的持久记录，但其定量重用需要在制图地点、词汇标注与环境测量之间做出明确区分。现有工作常将计算所得的词汇检测视为已验证的词源，或将当代环境测量当作历史景观重建，缺乏严格、可复现且可追溯的整合方法。

Method: 数据集构建方法包括：采用可复现的聚落框架作为基础；基于冻结的 24 个威尔士地名要素注册表进行确定性词汇筛选（保留精确与前缀标记匹配及其来源）；通过稳定标识符关联聚落级环境属性（河流/海岸邻近度、高程、多尺度局部地形、土地覆盖、邻域木本覆盖，并使用独立的高程产品生成平行地形测量）；以四张关系表发布，并配有字段级数据字典、来源登记册与许可元数据。

Result: 数据集包含 3,757 条聚落记录、1,350 个词汇检测（覆盖 1,294 个聚落）。技术验证证实了关系完整性、确定性词汇重建的可复现性、环境覆盖的完备记录、独立地形测量来源之间的一致性强，以及冻结发布版本的完整复现。

Conclusion: TEMPLAR Wales 为地名学、语言地理学、历史与环境景观研究、GIS 和空间数据分析提供了可复用的基础，同时明确警示：不应将计算词汇检测视为已验证的词源，也不应将当代环境测量视为历史景观重建。

Abstract: Place names provide persistent records of how landscapes have been described and organised, but their quantitative reuse requires explicit separation between mapped places, lexical annotations and environmental measurements. TEMPLAR Wales is a georeferenced environmental-toponymy dataset comprising 3,757 settlement records across Wales. The resource links a reproducible settlement frame to deterministic lexical screening and settlement-level environmental attributes through stable identifiers. It contains 1,350 lexical detections across 1,294 settlements, generated from a frozen registry of 24 Welsh place-name elements, while retaining exact- and prefix-token matches and their provenance separately. Environmental attributes describe river and coastal proximity, elevation and local terrain context at multiple spatial scales, land cover and neighbourhood woody cover, with parallel terrain measurements derived from independent elevation products. The dataset is distributed as four relational tables accompanied by a field-level data dictionary, source-provenance register and licensing metadata. Technical validation confirms relational integrity, deterministic lexical reconstruction, documented environmental coverage, strong agreement between independent terrain sources and reproducible reconstruction of the frozen release. TEMPLAR Wales provides a reusable foundation for research in toponymy, linguistic geography, historical and environmental landscape studies, GIS and spatial data analysis without treating computational lexical detections as verified etymologies or contemporary environmental measurements as historical landscape reconstructions.

</details>


### [362] [Terrain signatures in Welsh settlement names](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26978)
*Oktay Karakuş,Can Eyupoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过分析3,757个威尔士聚落地名，该研究使用预注册模型和地理空间验证，发现带有高地地形元素（bryn/mynydd）的地名平均比其2公里周边环境高出24.4米，且该关联在独立高程数据源和不同空间邻域定义下得到复现；将地名地形极性加入基线模型可将空间留出验证的均方误差降低4.63%–7.30%。但研究明确限定结论：地名类别保留了关于当代地形的可测量信息，并未确立个体词源、因果命名、历史环境记忆或对其他命名系统的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 景观地名的语义在多大程度上保留可测量的环境信息（而非仅反映广阔的地理结构）很少被严格检验。作者旨在用冻结的、来源审计的词汇框架、预注册模型和地理结构化验证，对威尔士聚落地名与当代地形高程之间的关系进行严格因果无关但预测性的评估。

Method: 使用24元素的冻结词汇框架分析3,757个威尔士聚落；核心对比为101个高地地形名（bryn/mynydd）与139个低地地形名（cwm/pant）的聚落；采用预注册的结果特定模型，在1、2、5公里三种预设邻域定义下测量相对周围地形的高程差；以独立高程数据源复现；并通过10、25、50公里空间分块的留出验证，比较加入地形名称极性前后的均方误差来评估增量预测价值。

Result: 高地地形名聚落相对其2公里周边平均高出24.4米（95% CI 10.8–38.1 m；Holm校正p=0.00137），且关联在1、2、5公里邻域定义下均为正；用独立高程源复现结果为24.1米（95% CI 10.6–37.6 m）。加入地形名极性使非词汇基线的空间留出均方误差分别降低4.63%、6.22%和7.30%（对应10/25/50公里分块），但改进在各留出区域间存在差异。河流相关地名证据较弱但方向一致；预注册的林地模型不可估计。

Conclusion: 选定的威尔士聚落地名类别确实保留了关于威尔士境内当代地形的可测量信息，但这并不确立个体词源、因果命名关系、历史环境记忆，也不表明结论可迁移到其他命名系统。残余空间结构、未解决的地名语言问题及缺乏独立外部复现限制了解释的强度。

Abstract: Landscapes are named, but whether names retain measurable environmental information beyond broad geographic structure is rarely tested. We analysed 3,757 Welsh settlements using a frozen, source-audited 24-element lexical framework, preregistered outcome-specific models and geographically structured validation. The central comparison contrasted 101 settlements carrying high-terrain elements (\textit{bryn} or \textit{mynydd}) with 139 carrying low-terrain elements (\textit{cwm} or \textit{pant}). High-terrain names occupied locations 24.4 m higher relative to their 2-km surroundings (95\% CI, 10.8--38.1 m; Holm-adjusted $p$ = 0.00137). The association remained positive across prespecified 1-, 2- and 5-km neighbourhood definitions and was reproduced using an independently produced elevation source (24.1 m; 95\% CI, 10.6--37.6 m). Adding terrain-name polarity to a non-lexical spatial and settlement baseline reduced geographically held-out mean squared error by 4.63\%, 6.22\% and 7.30\% under 10-, 25- and 50-km spatial blocking, respectively, although improvement varied among held-out regions. River-related names provided weaker, directionally consistent evidence, while the preregistered woodland model was non-estimable. Residual spatial structure, unresolved name language and the absence of independent external replication limit interpretation. Selected Welsh settlement-name categories therefore retain measurable information about present-day terrain within Wales, without establishing individual etymology, causal naming, historical environmental memory or transferability to other naming systems.

</details>


### [363] [Decentralized Multitask Learning over Learned Task Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26989)
*Zirui Wan,Stefan Vlaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究网络上的去中心化多任务学习问题，提出了一种两阶段策略：先从噪声非协作随机梯度迭代中估计广义图拉普拉斯算子，再利用学习到的任务图实现协作多任务扩散学习。理论分析量化了拉普拉斯估计误差及其对多任务扩散递归稳态性能的影响，并引入拓扑敏感性指标；仿真实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图正则化多任务学习框架通常假设任务间关系（图结构）已知，但在实际应用中任务间的依赖关系往往未知，需要直接从分布式数据中学习。因此需要一种既能去中心化执行又能自动学习任务依赖关系的多任务学习框架。

Method: 提出去中心化两阶段策略：第一阶段从带噪声的非协作随机梯度迭代中估计广义图拉普拉斯算子，其推导基于高斯马尔可夫随机场先验，得到图拉普拉斯的去中心化最大似然估计器；第二阶段利用学习到的任务图进行协作多任务扩散学习。分析中引入拓扑敏感性指数以刻画网络异质性的影响。

Result: 理论分析量化了拉普拉斯估计误差以及该误差向多任务扩散递归稳态性能的传播；仿真结果表明，基于学习任务图的协作学习相比非协作学习显著提升了性能，且在估计步长足够小时可逼近真实图基线的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段去中心化策略能够在任务关系未知的情况下有效学习任务图并利用其提升多任务学习性能，接近已知真实结构时的基线水平，为去中心化多任务学习提供了理论保证与实用方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates decentralized multitask learning over networks when the underlying task relationships are unknown. While existing graph-regularized multitask frameworks typically assume a known structure, practical settings often require learning inter-task dependencies directly from distributed data. We propose a decentralized two-phase strategy that first estimates a generalized graph Laplacian from noisy non-cooperative stochastic gradient iterates, and subsequently exploits the learned graph to enable cooperative multitask diffusion learning. This framework is motivated by a Gaussian Markov random field prior, which gives rise to a decentralized maximum likelihood estimator for the graph Laplacian. The analysis quantifies the Laplacian estimation error and its propagation to the steady-state performance of the multitask diffusion recursion, and introduces a topology sensitivity index to capture the effect of network heterogeneity. Simulation results corroborate the theoretical findings and demonstrate that cooperation enabled by the learned task graph significantly improves performance over non-cooperative learning, while approaching the true-graph baseline when the estimation stepsize is sufficiently small.

</details>


### [364] [Benchmarking_Fast_Domain_Adaptation_for_Unsupervised_Speech_Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26992)
*Robin San Roman,Manel Khentout,Tu Anh Nguyen,Paul Michel,Yossi Adi,Emmanuel Dupoux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ABX-Accent，一个基于 AESRC 数据集、涵盖 10 种英语口音的基准，用于评估表示学习方法在口音（域外）语音上的表现及少样本域适应性。每个口音仅提供小于 10 小时的无标注训练数据，并沿用 Zero Resources Challenge 的 ABX 评估指标。文中以自适应域归一化微调预训练 Contrastive Predictive Coding (CPC) 模型作为基线，在 LibriSpeech 男/女划分上先验证方法，再迁移至新基准，平均跨说话人 ABX 分数相对提升 23.6%。数据和指标将在论文录用后开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管表示学习在预训练和下游任务中表现优异，但对域外（out-of-domain）语音的处理能力以及如何以少样本方式适应新域知之甚少。口音语音尤其关键，因为它呈现长尾分布、与标准口音差异显著，而现有基准缺乏对口音场景下表示学习泛化性与可适配性的系统评估。

Method: 构建 ABX-Accent 基准：基于 AESRC 数据集，覆盖 10 种英语口音，每个口音提供小于 10 小时的无标注训练集，并将 Zero Resources Challenge 的 ABX 评估指标适配到各口音。基线方法采用自适应域归一化（adaptive domain normalization），对预训练 CPC 模型按口音进行微调；方法先在 LibriSpeech 上的男/女划分中开发验证，再应用于新基准。

Result: 在 ABX-Accent 基准上，采用自适应域归一化的微调方法相比未适配模型，在跨说话人 ABX 分数上平均取得 23.6% 的相对提升。

Conclusion: 该工作证明即使是少量无标注口音数据，结合域自适应微调也能显著改善预训练表示模型的口音泛化能力；ABX-Accent 可作为口音场景下评估与改进无监督表示学习方法的标准化基准，数据与指标将开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Representation learning has attracted great atten- tion and managed to reach good performances as a pretraining method for downstream tasks or as a first step towards unsu- pervised speech modeling. Yet, little is known about how such methods deal with out-of-domain speech and how could they be adapted in a few shot to new domains. This is important especially for accented speech where one observes a long tail of accents that diverge from the standard ones. We introduce ABX- Accent, a benchmark based on the AESRC dataset that features 10 different accents of English. It includes a small (< 10 hours) unlabelled training set in each of the accents and adaptations of the Zero Resources Challenge ABX evaluation metrics to each of the accents. We illustrate this benchmark with a baseline model that uses adaptive domain normalization to fine tune a pretrained Contrastive Predictive Coding model on the accents. This method is first developed on LibriSpeech using a male/female split. When applied to the new benchmark, the proposed method yields a relative improvement of 23.6% on across-speaker ABX scores on average compared to non adapted models. The data and metrics will be open sourced upon paper acceptance

</details>


### [365] [Disentangling Optimization Scale from Preference Scale in DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27032)
*Ivan Kruzhilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出DPO中的系数β实际上纠缠了两个独立作用：控制逆偏好噪声尺度与缩放优化动力学（有效步长）。这导致在固定学习率下策略偏离对β呈非单调关系（小β时死区、中等β达峰、大β下降），且不同β下的loss不可直接比较。作者提出与DPO在β>0时argmin等价的centered-softplus重构，将两个效应解耦并可独立调节，同时该归一化目标在β→0时退化为线性偏好边界目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO是主流偏好对齐目标，其中β通常被解释为控制对参考策略的KL约束强度。作者发现这一解读不准确：β同时决定逆偏好噪声尺度和优化动态缩放，二者纠缠使β的角色被遮蔽，导致对超参数选择更敏感、学习率调度复杂化，并妨碍跨β的loss比较。

Method: 作者首先从理论上分析，将β拆解为作用在损失函数尺度（逆偏好噪声）与梯度/优化动态（有效步长）上的两重功能，解释固定学习率下策略偏离的非单调现象。随后提出centered-softplus重构版本，该目标与标准DPO在β>0时共享相同的argmin（最优策略），但将逆噪声尺度和学习率效应显式分离、独立可调；其归一化形式还具备β→0的连续极限，退化为线性偏好边距目标。

Result: 理论分析表明：在固定学习率下策略与参考模型的KL偏离随β先消失（小β死区）、后达峰、再下降；标准DPO loss在不同β下不可比，即使loss曲线几乎一致，KL偏离也可能相差数倍。centered-softplus重构在保持最优策略不变的前提下解耦了β的双重作用，并给出β→0的连续端点。

Conclusion: β在DPO中不应仅被视为KL约束系数，其双重作用导致了反直觉的动态行为与超参数敏感性。centered-softplus重构通过解耦逆噪声尺度和优化动态，使两者的作用显式且独立可调，改善了可解释性、超参数稳健性和学习率调度，并自然扩展到线性边距损失的极限情形。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a widely used objective for aligning language models from preference data, with the coefficient $β$ commonly interpreted as controlling the KL constraint to a reference policy. We show that $β$ entangles two distinct roles: it governs the effective inverse preference-noise scale and simultaneously rescales the optimization dynamics, coupling this scale with the effective step size. As a consequence, at a fixed learning rate the achieved policy deviation is non-monotone in $β$: it vanishes in a dead zone at small $β$, reaches a peak at an intermediate value, and decreases again for larger $β$. Moreover, standard DPO loss values are not comparable across $β$: runs with nearly identical loss curves can differ several-fold in KL divergence from the reference model. This entanglement obscures the role of $β$, increases sensitivity to hyperparameter choices, and complicates learning-rate scheduling. We propose a centered-softplus reformulation that is argmin-equivalent to DPO for $β>0$, while making the inverse preference-noise-scale and learning-rate effects explicit and independently tunable. The normalized centered-softplus objective also admits a continuous $β\to0$ endpoint that reduces to a linear preference-margin objective.

</details>


### [366] [Performance Foundations of Parallel & Distributed Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27046)
*Maciej Besta,Leonard Schmidt,Lara Nonino,Robert Gerstenberger,Pierre Pang,Patrik Okanovic,Ales Kubicek,Tiancheng Chen,Baraq Lipshitz,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）及RL式后训练范式，从计算与并行系统角度剖析了PPO、GRPO及其变体等主流RL-for-LLM算法框架，并提出一套基于工作-深度（work-depth）模型的系统化分类法，涵盖数据、张量、流水线、序列、上下文、专家等传统并行策略，以及分解部署、阶段融合、混合并行和异步执行等新型多模型RLM训练并行技术。作者在分析现有RLM框架的基础上，提炼了构建高性能、可扩展且成本高效的推理语言模型（RLM）的实践指南，并指出了开放研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DeepSeek-R1、o3、Kimi k1.5等RLM通过RL式后训练大幅提升了链式推理、长程规划与自我纠错能力，但这类训练的计算开销极其巨大（需数百万GPU小时和多模型紧耦合流水线），远超经典监督LLM训练。RLM训练因而既是算法问题，也是并行与分布式系统问题。当前缺乏对该范式系统性、以计算为核心的分析，作者旨在填补这一空白，推动研发高性能、可扩展且成本高效的RLM。

Method: 作者首先对RL-for-LLM范式进行系统化整理，并对PPO、GRPO及其变体等主流后训练算法框架做以计算为中心的剖析；其次，建立面向RL-for-LLM的模型内与模型间并行策略分类法，覆盖传统并行技术（数据、张量、流水线、序列、上下文、专家并行）及多模型RLM训练的新型并行与优化技术（如分解放置、阶段融合、混合并行和异步执行）；最后利用并行计算中的工作-深度模型为分类法提供严格且可移植的理论基础，并据此分析现有RLM框架。

Result: 本文产出了系统的RL-for-LLM计算分析、涵盖传统与新型技术的并行策略分类法，以及基于工作-深度模型的严格理论框架。基于对现有RLM框架的分析，作者提炼出可操作的实践指南，并明确了面向可扩展、快速、成本高效RLM构建的开放研究方向。

Conclusion: 实现高性能、可扩展且成本高效的RLM，必须将RL-for-LLM训练视为并行与分布式系统问题而非单纯算法问题；通过将并行策略（含多模型新型并行技术）系统化并以工作-深度模型加以严格化，可为未来RLM训练系统的设计与优化提供理论依据和实践指导，同时仍有许多开放问题有待探索。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and other RL-style post-training paradigms have been used for aligning large language models (LLMs) with reasoning standards. The resulting recent Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) such as DeepSeek-R1, o3, and Kimi k1.5 show that such RL-style post-training ("RL-for-LLMs") can substantially improve chain-of-thought reasoning, long-horizon planning, and self-correction. However, the computational footprint of these systems is massive: state-of-the-art RLM training requires millions of GPU-hours and tightly coupled multi-model pipelines that stress modern hardware far beyond classical supervised LLM training. This makes RLM training as much a parallel and distributed systems problem as an algorithmic one. In this work, to facilitate developing RLMs that are simultaneously high-performance, scalable, and cost-effective, we first systematize the RL-for-LLM paradigm and provide a compute-centric analysis of prominent post-training algorithmic frameworks: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), as well as their variants. Second, we develop a taxonomy of intra- and inter-model parallelism strategies for RL-for-LLMs, covering both traditional techniques (data, tensor, pipeline, sequence, context, and expert parallelism) as well as novel forms of parallelism and optimization techniques for multi-model RLM training, for example disaggregated placement, stage fusion, hybrid parallelism, and asynchronous execution. We harness the work-depth model of parallel computing to make our taxonomy and its insights rigorous and portable. Finally, we analyze existing RLM frameworks and we distill practical guidelines and outline open research directions for building scalable, fast, and cost-effective RLMs.

</details>


### [367] [Soft Active Electromyography Interface for Machine Learning-Enabled Silent Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27048)
*Yuta Kurotaki,Shusuke Yamakoshi,Reitaro Yoshida,Yutaka Isoda,Tamami Takano,Yuji Isano,Yusuke Miyake,Kentaro Kuribayashi,Hiroki Ota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种戴在手上的软性主动肌电（EMG）接口，通过指尖电极在需要时靠近嘴唇采集肌电信号，结合液态金属互连、透明柔性印刷电路（FPC）电极和弹性体封装保证机械稳定性，并用深度神经网络对30词词汇分类，跨3名受试者平均准确率达97.2±1.3%，且通过实时无人机控制验证了其在嘈杂和隐私敏感环境中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无声语音识别（SSR）可在无 audible 语音时提供替代通信途径，但传统方法受限于需持续贴附面部、隐私担忧以及信号采集不稳定等问题，因此需要一种按需使用、稳定且隐私友好的采集方案。

Method: 提出一种软性主动EMG接口：佩戴在手上，指尖电极可仅在需要时靠近嘴唇采集肌电信号；接口集成液态金属互连、透明柔性印刷电路电极和弹性体封装，以确保手指运动时的高机械稳定性；利用在这些稳定信号上训练的深度神经网络进行词级单词分类。

Result: 深度神经网络在3名受试者上对30词词汇分类的平均准确率为97.2±1.3%，展现出稳健的语言学判别能力；此外，实时无人机控制验证了该方法在嘈杂、隐私敏感环境下（传统语音识别失效的场景）的实用性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，软性可穿戴EMG系统有望成为安全、直观的人机交互界面，弥补传统语音识别在隐私和噪声场景中的不足。

Abstract: Silent speech recognition (SSR) provides an alternative communication pathway in the absence of audible speech. However, conventional approaches are limited by the need for constant facial attachment, privacy concerns, and unstable signal acquisition. Here, we propose a soft, active electromyography (EMG) interface that enables word-level SSR using machine learning. Worn on the hand, the device uses a fingertip electrode that can be positioned near the lips to acquire EMG signals only when needed. The interface integrates liquid metal (LM) interconnects, transparent flexible printed circuit (FPC) electrodes, and elastomer encapsulation to ensure high mechanical stability during finger motion. A deep neural network trained on these stable signals achieved a mean accuracy of 97.2 $\pm$ 1.3% across three subjects in classifying a 30-word vocabulary, demonstrating robust linguistic discrimination. Furthermore, real-time drone control validates the practicality of this approach in noisy and privacy-sensitive environments where conventional voice recognition fails. This study highlights the potential of soft, wearable EMG systems as secure and intuitive human-machine interfaces.

</details>


### [368] [Unifying Detection and Adaptation in Task-Free Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27070)
*Dezheng Han,Anbang Zhang,Zhihao Zhu,Shuaishuai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FiUni（Fisher-guided unified）框架，面向大规模语言模型的无任务边界（task-free）持续学习，利用预训练模型 Fisher 信息矩阵的 K-FAC 近似主子空间正交性来检测批级任务，并以 Fisher 导出的冻结子空间引导 LoRA 自适应地进行知识复用、子空间扩展或新建，从而动态平衡知识共享与任务隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法通常依赖显式任务边界来约束参数更新或引入任务适配模块，难以适用于真实的无任务边界场景；同时下游持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题亟需缓解。

Method: FiUni 基于一个关键观测：由少量下游任务样本估计的预训练模型 K-FAC 近似 Fisher 信息矩阵的主子空间之间的正交性能反映任务相似性。据此，FiUni 构造 FIM 派生的冻结子空间以指导低秩适配（LoRA），并将每个输入批窗口的 Fisher 主子空间与历史子空间匹配，自适应地决定复用已有知识、扩展相关子空间或创建新子空间。

Result: 实验表明 FiUni 能有效推断隐性的批级任务归属，并以更少的可训练参数取得与先进的任务感知持续学习方法相当的竞争性能。

Conclusion: FiUni 通过 FIM 主子空间相似性度量实现无任务边界的批级任务检测与参数高效持续适配，动态平衡知识共享与任务隔离，为真实场景下的 LLM 持续学习提供了一种无需任务标签的可行方案。

Abstract: To mitigate catastrophic forgetting in downstream continual learning (CL) for large language models (LLMs), existing methods typically constrain parameter updates or introduce task-specific adaptation modules. However, these methods often rely on explicit task boundaries during training, limiting their applicability to realistic task-free scenarios. In this paper, we propose a \textbf{Fi}sher-guided \textbf{uni}fied (\textbf{FiUni}) framework for batch-level task detection and parameter-efficient continual adaptation. FiUni is motivated by a key observation about the Fisher information matrix (FIM) of pre-trained models: the orthogonality among the principal subspaces of its Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature (K-FAC) approximation, estimated from a small number of downstream task samples, can reflect the similarity between different tasks. Based on this observation, FiUni constructs FIM-derived frozen subspaces to guide low-rank adaptation (LoRA), while matching the Fisher principal subspace of each incoming batch window with historical subspaces. This enables FiUni to adaptively determine whether to reuse existing knowledge, expand a related subspace, or create a new subspace, dynamically balancing knowledge sharing and task isolation. Experiments show that FiUni can effectively infer latent batch-level task affiliations and achieve competitive performance against advanced task-aware CL methods with fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [369] [Emotional Preferences as Goal-Priority Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27072)
*Shiqi Liu,Yihua Tan,Hu Fu,Guanyu Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“涌现情绪偏好”的概念，通过强化学习实现由高层目标自主生成的状态依赖偏好，用以动态调节多目标强化学习框架中竞争性底层目标之间的相对优先级。框架由内部控制器（提供偏好条件下的目标导向行为库）与外部偏好生成器（学习状态到目标偏好的映射）组成。作者刻画了偏好调节策略空间，推导了以内部行为库表示误差为变量的最优性差距上界，并在多目标探索环境中验证了该方法具有上下文优先级切换、分级权衡和时间持续性，且优于固定偏好与手工偏好策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体决策中的一个核心问题是：竞争性底层目标的相对优先级能否由高层目标自主生成的“情绪偏好”来决定，而非由外部预先指定。在外部环境变化和内部状态演化下，情绪在调节竞争目标相对优先级方面具有重要的功能作用。受目标导向情绪理论启发，本文探究如何通过强化学习在计算上实现这种偏好调节机制。

Method: 提出“涌现情绪偏好”这一概念：高层目标自主诱导出对竞争性底层目标的状态依赖偏好。该概念建立在一个双层次框架之上：内部为多目标强化学习控制器，提供一组偏好条件下的目标导向行为库（repertoire）；外部为偏好生成器，通过针对高层目标的强化学习学习从当前状态到目标偏好的映射。情绪偏好被操作化为通过优化涌现出的目标相对优先级的状态依赖调节。此外，论文刻画了偏好调节所诱导的策略空间，并以内部行为库的表示误差为目标推导了最优性差距的上界；当最优策略可由现有偏好条件策略表示时，该差距趋于零。

Result: 在自构建的多目标探索环境中的实验表明，学习到的偏好函数展现出上下文优先级切换、分级权衡和时间持续性等特征，并且在性能上优于所评估的固定偏好策略和手工偏好策略。

Conclusion: 作者认为，情绪偏好可作为高层目标自主调节底层目标优先级的计算机制，通过强化学习使偏好调节自然涌现，且在理论上（最优性差距上界及其可消失条件）与实践上（优于固定/手工偏好）均证明了该方法的有效性，为情绪功能在智能体决策中的计算实现提供了支持。

Abstract: A core question in decision-making for agents is whether the relative priorities of competing lower-level objectives can be determined by emotional preferences autonomously generated by higher-level goals, rather than being externally prespecified. Under changing external environments and evolving internal states, emotions play an important functional role in regulating the relative priorities of competing goals. Inspired by the goal-directed theory of emotion, this paper studies how such preference regulation can be computationally realized through reinforcement learning. We first propose a conception of emergent emotional preference: a high-level goal autonomously induces state-dependent preferences over competing lower-level objectives. This conception is built upon a framework consisting of a multi-objective reinforcement learning inner controller and an outer preference generator. The inner controller provides a repertoire of preference-conditioned goal-directed behaviors, while the outer preference generator learns a mapping from the current state to objective preferences through reinforcement learning on a high-level goal. We operationalize emotional preference as a state-dependent regulation of relative goal priorities that emerges through optimization. Furthermore, we characterize the policy space induced by preference regulation and derive an upper bound on the optimality gap in terms of the representation error of the inner behavioral repertoire. We show that the gap vanishes when the optimal policy can be represented by the available preference-conditioned policies. Experiments in self-constructed multi-objective exploration environments show that the learned preference function exhibits contextual priority switching, graded trade-offs, and temporal persistence, and outperforms the evaluated fixed-preference and handcrafted-preference strategies.

</details>


### [370] [Tabular Deep Learning for Algorithmic Trading: Cross-Regime Bayesian Optimisation for Equity Signal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27076)
*Joshua Le Grice*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了算法交易中股票预测模型的超参数选择对市场制度（regime）鲁棒性的影响。在约300只美国大盘股、11年的日度数据上比较了五类模型，并用贝叶斯优化针对三种统计上不同的市场制度进行交易性能优化。结果表明，制度鲁棒的超参数选择可带来样本外泛化能力；没有任何表格型深度学习模型能超越梯度提升树，但通过秩聚合组合XGBoost与TabNet得到的混合集成实现了年化收益51.26%、夏普比率2.44及统计显著的CAPM alpha（0.423，p=0.011），且beta接近于零，表明超额收益来自选股而非市场暴露。另类数据在技术面与基本面特征之外仅起次要作用，且在空头侧贡献更强。文中还提供了交互式可视化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法交易市场规模超过200亿美元，信号鲁棒性的微小提升即可带来可观的经济回报。然而，现有股票预测模型的评估在超参数选择过程中并未显式针对市场制度（regime）鲁棒性进行优化，本文旨在填补这一空白，检验制度鲁棒的超参数选择能否改善样本外泛化与组合表现。

Method: 使用约300只美国大盘股、跨越11年的日度观测数据训练五类模型（含梯度提升树与表格型深度学习架构），通过贝叶斯优化（Bayesian optimisation）将超参数选择目标设为在三个统计上不同的市场制度下的交易性能。构建制度鲁棒的超参数选择流程，并采用秩聚合（rank aggregation）组合XGBoost与TabNet形成混合集成。评估维度包括信号精度、组合绩效、模拟输入噪声下的退化行为、以及另类数据特征对多空两侧的边际贡献。

Result: 制度鲁棒的超参数选择与样本外泛化相关：测试期四个季度内信号精度始终高于随机基线；组合绩效在模拟输入噪声下缓慢退化，超过一定阈值后才崩溃。无任何单一表格型深度学习架构优于梯度提升树，但XGBoost与TabNet的秩聚合混合集成年化收益为51.26%，夏普比率2.44，CAPM alpha为0.423（p=0.011）且统计显著，beta接近零，表明超额收益源于选股能力。另类数据在技术面和基本面特征之外作用次要，对空头侧贡献强于多头侧，且贡献因模型类别而异。

Conclusion: 制度鲁棒的贝叶斯超参数选择能够提升股票预测模型在未知市场条件下的泛化能力；表格型深度学习与梯度提升树之间并非非此即彼，混合集成策略可融合二者优势取得显著超额收益（且不依赖市场暴露）。另类数据应被视为增量信息而非核心信号源。研究者已构建交互式应用以实时探索结果，后续仅需接入实时数据即可向实际部署迈进。

Abstract: Algorithmic trading now represents a market exceeding $20 billion, where even marginal gains in signal robustness can translate into economically significant returns. Existing evaluations of equity prediction models do not explicitly target regime robustness during hyperparameter selection. Five model classes are trained on daily observations from approximately 300 large-cap US equities over eleven years, with Bayesian optimisation configured to target trading performance across three statistically different market regimes. Regime-robust hyperparameter selection is associated with out-of-sample generalisation, as signal precision remains above the random baseline across all four quarters of the test period, and portfolio performance slowly degrades under simulated input noise before collapsing beyond a defined threshold. No individual tabular deep learning architecture outperforms gradient-boosted trees, but combining XGBoost and TabNet using rank aggregation produces a Hybrid ensemble with an annualised return of 51.26%, a Sharpe ratio of 2.44, and a statistically significant CAPM alpha of 0.423 (p = 0.011). A near-zero beta indicates this outperformance is driven by stock selection, not market exposure. Alternative data plays a secondary role once technical and fundamental features are accounted for, as well as contributing more strongly on the short side than the long, and varies by model class. An interactive application makes these results explorable in real time, with live data integration the remaining step toward practical deployment.

</details>


### [371] [Cone Extended Rayleigh Quotients for Directed Graph Learning: Minimax Spectral Certificates, Sensitivity, and Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27122)
*Yavdat Sh. Il'yasov,Nur F. Valeev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向有向图学习的谱分析方法论，基于双锥Rayleigh框架处理广义束 B_θ-λG，无需对称性、非负性或锥不变性即可进行谱认证、灵敏度和控制，并给出可微的一阶最优干预方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图学习天然产生不可对称化的非对称传播算子，其左右谱结构不同，而现有谱工具多依赖对称/非负/锥不变假设，难以直接用于可训练的非对称算子。

Method: 基于广义束 B_θ-λG 的双侧锥Rayleigh框架，发展面向学习的谱认证方法；在正象限下给出可计算的下/上锥界用于事后再包络；用光滑soft-min/max代理保持严格单侧界并给出显式逼近误差且对参数可微；对简单内点建立 Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C 的左右模式灵敏度公式，支持预算约束下的一阶最优图干预。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法可超出锥保持算子和有向学习设置应用；带符号的非对称扰动显示内点特征对到边界互补拟对的转变（含非谱锥水平）；对称化实验表明可去除仅由边方向携带的预测信息；在Cora引文网络上，自适应重算左右灵敏度在累计0.5%边权缩减预算下将判定谱水平降低约21.5%，且测试精度无变化。

Conclusion: 该方法为有向学习中的非对称算子提供了一套无需对称/非负/锥不变的谱分析、灵敏度与自适应控制框架，并展示了其在实际有向图学习中的有效性与方向性信息的不可替代性。

Abstract: Directed graph learning naturally leads to trainable nonsymmetric propagation operators with distinct right and left spectral structures. Building on the two-sided cone Rayleigh framework for generalized pencils \[ B_θ-λG, \] we develop a learning-oriented methodology for spectral certification, sensitivity analysis, and control without requiring symmetry, nonnegativity, or cone preservation. In the positive-orthant setting, computable lower and upper cone bounds provide an a posteriori enclosure of a distinguished cone level, while smooth soft-min/max surrogates preserve rigorous one-sided bounds with explicit approximation errors and remain differentiable with respect to the trainable parameters.
  For a simple interior level, the right and left modes satisfy \[ Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C, \] yielding first-order optimal graph-supported interventions under prescribed perturbation budgets and motivating adaptive spectral control. Numerical experiments demonstrate the applicability of the approach beyond cone-preserving operators and in directed learning settings. Signed nonsymmetric perturbations reveal a transition from interior eigenpairs to boundary complementary quasi-pairs, including non-spectral cone levels, while controlled experiments show that symmetrization can remove predictive information carried solely by edge direction.
  On the directed Cora citation network, adaptive recomputation of the right--left sensitivity reduces the distinguished spectral level by approximately $21.5\%$ under a cumulative edge-weight reduction budget of $0.5\%$, with no observed change in test accuracy for the trained model and data split considered.

</details>


### [372] [TRACE-CRC: Trajectory-Adaptive Conformal Risk Control for Multi-Step Channel State Information Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27124)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Mehdi Sattari,Javad Aliakbari,Tommy Svensson,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE-CRC，一种面向多步信道状态信息（CSI）预测的轨迹感知不确定性量化方法。它围绕预测的CSI矩阵构建Frobenius范数不确定性球，并通过结合未来步相关误差画像、轨迹难度分层与"学习-然后-测试"（LTT）风险控制，保证至少一个未来帧未被覆盖的风险受控，实现了可靠的轨迹级覆盖率，同时比保守的多步校正方法产生更小的不确定性球，并避免紧凑逐步式与自适应共形基准的轨迹欠覆盖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的时变CSI预测对高效无线通信至关重要，但现代基于深度学习的CSI预测器通常只提供点预测，缺乏校准的不确定性估计。这个问题在多步CSI预测中尤为突出——目标是一系列未来CSI矩阵，若预测轨迹的任何部分不可靠，波束成形、调度等下游决策就可能失败。现有方法要么对每步独立校准导致轨迹欠覆盖，要么采取保守的多步校正导致不确定性球过大。

Method: TRACE-CRC构建Frobenius范数不确定性球围绕预测的CSI矩阵，并控制"至少一个未来帧未被覆盖"的风险。与逐步独立校准不同，它融合了三项技术：(1) 依赖于未来步的误差画像（error profiling），刻画不同预测步的误差特性；(2) 轨迹难度分层（trajectory difficulty stratification），对不同难度的轨迹分别处理；(3) 基于"学习-然后-测试"（learn-then-test, LTT）的风险控制机制，用于整体调控轨迹级风险。

Result: 实验表明，TRACE-CRC在保持可靠轨迹级覆盖率的同时，产生的不确定性球显著小于保守的多步校正方法；相比紧凑的逐步式与自适应共形基线，又能避免轨迹欠覆盖问题。

Conclusion: TRACE-CRC实现了覆盖效率与可靠性之间的更优平衡，为多步CSI预测提供了一种轨迹感知的、经过校准的不确定性量化方案，从而支撑更稳健的下游无线通信决策。

Abstract: Reliable prediction of time-varying channel state information (CSI) is essential for efficient wireless communication. Each CSI frame is a matrix-valued representation of the wireless channel response, and a sequence of CSI frames forms a temporal channel trajectory. Modern deep learning-based CSI predictors, however, often provide only point predictions and lack calibrated uncertainty estimates. This limitation is particularly problematic in multi-step CSI prediction, where the target is a sequence of future CSI matrices, and downstream decisions such as beamforming or scheduling may fail if any part of the predicted trajectory is unreliable. We propose trajectory-adaptive calibration and error profiling with conformal risk control (TRACE-CRC), a method for trajectory-aware uncertainty quantification in multi-step CSI prediction. TRACE-CRC constructs Frobenius-norm uncertainty balls around predicted CSI matrices and controls the risk that at least one future frame is uncovered. Instead of calibrating each future step independently, TRACE-CRC combines future-step-dependent error profiling, trajectory difficulty stratification, and learn-then-test (LTT) risk control. Empirically, TRACE-CRC achieves reliable trajectory-level coverage with substantially smaller uncertainty balls than conservative multi-step corrections, while avoiding the trajectory undercoverage of compact stepwise and adaptive conformal baselines.

</details>


### [373] [Ultra Low-Power, Lightweight, Probabilistic RSS-Based Path Reconstruction: A System for Landscape-Scale Bee Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27152)
*Christopher J. Noroozi,Joseph L. Woodgate,Michael Mangan,Michael T. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于接收信号强度（RSS）的新型定位方法，利用少量RSS测量和旋转高增益发射机，结合高斯过程与双随机变分推断，对超轻量、低功耗（38mg接收机、<180µW功耗）的移动接收机进行约15m精度的路径重建，额外测量可将精度提升至约10m（<600µW），并演示了对熊蜂归巢飞行的追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动生态学、物联网和机器人等领域需要为太小、功耗受限而无法使用GNSS的设备提供定位。现有基于RSS推断到达角（AoA）的低功耗定位方法受限于范围和大量RSS测量带来的功耗负担。

Method: 采用简单的旋转高增益发射机（300m范围），用最少量的RSS测量推断AoA，通过概率建模估计到达角；接收机运动路径用高斯过程建模，并通过双随机变分推断进行重建。

Result: 对仅38mg（含电源）的接收机在可扩展景观范围内实现约15m的追踪精度，功耗低于180µW；通过增加RSS测量可将精度提升至约10m，功耗低于600µW。该系统成功应用于追踪熊蜂（Bombus terrestris）归巢飞行。

Conclusion: 该方法有望支持飞行昆虫行为研究等需要超轻量、低功耗定位的领域，为GNSS受限的小型设备提供低功耗、可扩展的定位方案。

Abstract: Applications in fields such as movement ecology, Internet of Things or robotics share the need for systems that localize devices that are too small and power constrained to implement GNSS (Global Navigation Satellite Systems). Alternative low-power localization methods often rely on only measurements of RSS (Received Signal Strength) to infer the AoA (Angle of Arrival) of a transmitted radio frequency signal, but are limited by range and the power demand of the large number of RSS measurements required to infer an accurate AoA. In this paper we address these issues with a novel RSS-based method for tracking ultra lightweight and low-power moving receivers across a complex landscape, achieved by using a minimal number of RSS measurements from simple rotating high-gain transmitters with a range of 300m, and applying probabilistic modelling to infer their AoA. The receiver's movement path is then modelled using a Gaussian process and reconstructed using doubly stochastic variational inference, resulting in approximately 15m accuracy tracking of receivers weighing 38mg (including power source) over a scalable landscape range while consuming less than 180uW, increased to approximately 10m accuracy at less than 600uW by taking more RSS measurements. We anticipate that this method will support fields such as the behavioural study of flying insect species, which we demonstrate by applying the system to track Bombus terrestris nest return flights.

</details>


### [374] [Inductive Correlation Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27153)
*Francesco Paolo Nerini,Francesco Bonchi,Arijit Khan,André Panisson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用图神经网络（GNN）解决归纳式相关聚类（Inductive Correlation Clustering）问题——这是相关聚类（CC）的一个新推广，使模型经过训练后能直接处理未见过的图实例，无需重新计算。方法在学习阶段利用常见结构模式与节点特征，在新图上仅需极小的计算开销即可完成聚类。实验表明，该框架在归纳场景下推理时间最多降低5个数量级，同时近似比保持在与最优基线方案约10%以内的差距；在传统（直推式）CC基准上也表现优异。此外，该方法还可作为可学习的池化机制用于图分类，增强GNN捕获网络层级结构信息的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关聚类（CC）是组合优化中一种自然的聚类形式，虽通用性强，但现有算法存在两大缺陷：一是大规模数据上可扩展性差；二是本质上是直推式的，即每个新问题实例都必须从头重新运行算法。作者希望弥合这一差距，让聚类能够泛化到未见过的图实例。

Method: 引入GNN来求解归纳式相关聚类（Inductive Correlation Clustering）：在训练阶段学习利用输入图的公共结构模式与节点特征，从而基于从同一分布中抽取的新图进行泛化，推理时计算开销显著低于标准算法。该方法还被设计用作图分类中可学习的池化机制。

Result: 大量实验验证了框架的有效性与可扩展性：在归纳设置下推理时间最多降低5个数量级，且近似比保持在最佳基线解约10%以内的水平；在标准（直推式）CC基准上也取得有竞争力的结果。作为图分类的池化层时，能有效提升GNN捕获网络层级结构信息的能力。

Conclusion: 基于GNN的归纳式相关聚类框架在效率和泛化能力上显著优于传统算法，兼具直推式基准上的竞争力和实用的池化功能，为大规模聚类任务提供了一种高效、可扩展且具备学习能力的解决方案。

Abstract: Correlation Clustering (CC) is a natural formulation of clustering in combinatorial optimization, which uses a graph representation of the input and does not require a pre-specified number of clusters. Given $n$ objects and a pairwise similarity function, the goal is to cluster the objects so that similar objects are put in the same cluster and dissimilar objects are put in different clusters. Despite its versatility, existing CC algorithms suffer from significant scalability issues and are inherently transductive: i.e., the algorithm must be executed from scratch for any new problem instance.
  In this work, we bridge this gap by leveraging Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to solve Inductive Correlation Clustering, a novel generalization of the CC problem designed to handle unseen graph instances. By learning to exploit common structural patterns and node features during training, our framework generalizes to new graphs drawn from the same distribution with minimal computational overhead with respect to standard algorithms. We demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of our approach through extensive experiments. Our framework not only excels in the inductive setting, e.g., lowering the inference time up to $5$ order of magnitude, while maintaining an approximation ratio within $~10\%$ of the best baseline solution, but also achieves competitive results on standard (transductive) CC benchmarks. Finally, we showcase a practical application of our framework as a learnable pooling mechanism for graph classification. Our results indicate that our method serves as an efficient pooling layer, enhancing the ability of GNNs to capture hierarchical structural information in networks.

</details>


### [375] [Diffusion Policies for Short-Horizon Planning in Robot Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27158)
*Wendong Li,Jochen Garcke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个面向人群导航的离线到在线强化学习框架 PDPO，利用扩散策略生成短期动作块，并通过五步滚动时域方式执行；同时发现 benchmark 中的一个评估缺陷（无边界约束时智能体可离开有效区域绕过人群），因此将越界视为碰撞。实验表明 PDPO 在成功率上优于强基线，消融实验显示动作块在有界 benchmark 中尤为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人人群导航需要在密集、动态、多模态的人机交互下做出安全且高效的决策。现有强化学习方法在每个时间步仅输出单一反应性动作，无法充分表征多样化的短期避障策略，限制了决策的鲁棒性与灵活性。

Method: PDPO 包含两个阶段：(1) 离线阶段，在碰撞规避演示数据上预训练扩散策略；(2) 在线阶段，将去噪过程视为内部决策过程，用 PPO 进行在线微调。执行时策略生成五步动作块并以滚动时域（receding-horizon）方式应用。此外引入修改设定，将边界违反视为碰撞，以消除 benchmark 中智能体离开有效区域绕过密集人群的评估缺陷。

Result: 在人群导航 benchmark 上，PDPO 相较于强基线取得了更高的成功率；消融实验表明，动作块（action chunking）在引入边界约束的修改版 benchmark 中尤其重要。

Conclusion: 将扩散策略与动作块规划结合，并配合"越界即碰撞"的约束，可显著提升人群导航的学习性能，为多模态短期避障决策提供了一种有效的离线到在线训练范式。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires safe and efficient decision-making under dense, dynamic, and multimodal human--robot interactions. Existing reinforcement-learning methods typically output a single reactive action at each timestep, which limits their ability to represent diverse short-term avoidance strategies. We propose Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO), an offline-to-online reinforcement-learning framework that uses a diffusion policy to generate short-horizon action chunks for crowd navigation. PDPO is first pretrained on collision-avoidance demonstrations and then fine-tuned online with PPO by treating the denoising process as an internal decision process. During execution, the policy generates a five-step action chunk and applies it in a receding-horizon manner. Furthermore, we observe an evaluation artifact in common crowd-navigation benchmarks: without explicit boundary constraints, learned agents may leave the valid domain and bypass dense crowds. To address this, we introduce a setting in which boundary violations are treated as collisions. Experiments show that PDPO obtains an improved success rate over strong baselines, and ablations demonstrate that action chunks are especially important for the modified bounded benchmark.

</details>


### [376] [TraceBench: Controlled Evaluation of LLM Agents for Time-Series Root-Cause Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27182)
*Tommaso Bendinelli,Artur Dox,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TraceBench，一个基于仿真的可控根因归因任务生成框架，用于系统评估 LLM 智能体在时间序列异常检测与根因分析中的表现，并提供了数据集、智能体轨迹、实验结果与排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体正越来越多地被用于真实系统时间序列的异常检测与根因分析，但其在受控条件下的性能尚未被系统评估，缺乏可复现、可比较的基准。

Method: 构建 TraceBench 仿真框架：通过仿真物理动力学系统生成时间序列观测，每个任务要求智能体判断系统参数是否在仿真过程被改变以及具体是哪个参数；从三个可解释的机械系统生成任务，并在受控实验条件下系统评估四个 LLM 智能体。

Result: 智能体显著受益于领域上下文，且主要通过数值控制台输出来探索数据而非可视化；当要求智能体生成将每个时间序列样本映射到预测根因标签的 Python 脚本时，其表现普遍差于直接提交预测。

Conclusion: TraceBench 为可控条件下研究 LLM 智能体的时间序列根因分析提供了基准资源，研究结果揭示了智能体的探索与推理模式，相关数据集、智能体轨迹、实验结果和排行榜已在 tracebench.github.io 公开。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to anomaly detection and root-cause analysis in time-series observations collected from real-world systems; however, their performance on these tasks has not been systematically evaluated under controlled conditions. We introduce TraceBench, a simulation-based framework for generating controlled root-cause attribution tasks. In each generated task, an agent receives time-series observations produced by simulating a physical dynamical system and must determine whether a system parameter was altered during the simulation and, if so, which one. Using TraceBench, we generate tasks from three interpretable mechanical systems and systematically evaluate four LLM agents across controlled experimental conditions, yielding new insights into how these agents analyze time-series observations from dynamical systems. Our results show that agents benefit substantially from domain context and explore data primarily through numerical console output rather than visualizations. We also find that agents generally perform worse when required to produce a Python script that maps each time-series sample to a predicted root-cause label than when they submit predictions directly. We release our datasets, agent trajectories, experimental results, and a leaderboard on our website, tracebench.github.io.

</details>


### [377] [When Interference Graphs Evolve: Doubly Robust Estimation of Dynamic Peer Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27187)
*Xiaojing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对交互图动态演化时同伴效应难以估计的问题，提出一个"受控对比"（controlled contrast）框架：将潜在结果按自身处理、时间聚合的同伴暴露以及分配后网络演化摘要进行索引，由此定义自身处理效应、同伴暴露效应、受控网络演化效应和联合受控对比，而非进行中介分解。作者在此框架下开发了DynaNet-DR（动态网络双重稳健）估计量，结合时间因子化倾向得分与归一化增广；在一致性、摘要充分性、序贯可交换性、正则性、干扰收敛和弱依赖等条件下，只要结果回归或倾向得分估计器之一一致，其标准估计量即一致。半合成基准测试表明其在估计完整对比轮廓的方法中具有较好的精度；MathOverflow研究作为观测性示例展示了方法的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当交互图随时间演化时，同伴效应的估计面临特殊困难：分配前的网络历史、动态的同伴暴露和分配后的网络变化分别具有不同的因果作用，现有方法难以清晰区分这些机制，也缺少既能刻画动态暴露又能处理图演化的估计框架。

Method: 提出受控对比框架，将潜在结果按"自身处理水平、时间聚合的同伴暴露、分配后网络演化摘要"三重索引。在此基础上构建DynaNet-DR估计量：将倾向得分按时间因子化，并采用归一化增广（normalized augmentation）来结合结果回归与倾向得分信息；实现中加入了代表性得分预测、固定裁剪和有限样本稳定化技术。理论部分给出了所依赖的识别与推断条件（一致性、摘要充分性、序贯可交换性、正则性、干扰收敛、弱依赖）。

Result: 在固定真实时序图序列上的半合成基准测试中，DynaNet-DR在针对完整对比轮廓进行估计的各方法中表现出较好的估计精度；这些基准评估的是摘要索引对比而非反事实边生成。MathOverflow数据上的观测性研究在所述假设下给出了应用示例。

Conclusion: 受控对比框架与DynaNet-DR估计量能够对动态网络中自身处理、同伴暴露与网络演化三类因果作用进行一致估计，并具备双重稳健性质；该框架以摘要索引的对比为估计目标，避免了推断反事实边生成的不确定性，为动态网络同伴效应的实证研究提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Peer effects are difficult to estimate when interaction graphs evolve because pre-assignment network history, dynamic peer exposure, and post-assignment network change have distinct causal roles. We introduce a controlled contrast framework that indexes potential outcomes by own treatment, temporally aggregated peer exposure, and a post-assignment evolution summary. Differences between the resulting means define own-treatment, peer-exposure, controlled network-evolution, and joint controlled contrasts rather than a mediation decomposition. We develop the Dynamic Network Doubly Robust estimator, DynaNet-DR, which combines a temporally factorized propensity with normalized augmentation. Under consistency, summary sufficiency, sequential exchangeability, positivity, nuisance convergence, and weak dependence, its canonical estimator is consistent when either the outcome regression or the propensity estimator is consistent. The reported implementation adds representative-score prediction, fixed clipping, and finite-sample stabilization. Semi-synthetic benchmarks on fixed real temporal graph sequences show favorable estimation accuracy among methods targeting the full profile. These benchmarks assess summary-indexed contrasts rather than counterfactual edge generation, and the MathOverflow study is an observational illustration under the stated assumptions.

</details>


### [378] [Common Geodesics Do Not Guarantee Fisher Consistency of the Structured SVM: Minimal Counterexamples and a Tree-Metric Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27203)
*Jintao Fei,Jiangying Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化支持向量机（SSVM）的 Fisher 一致性。已知的必要条件要求任务损失是每个输出三元组都有公共测地点的度量，作者证明该条件对标准坐标式 argmax 解码器并非充分。他们完全分类了正加权树度量（argmax 一致性当且仅当树是路径），指出分支树上的失败局限于边界分布而全支撑分布下仍保持 argmax 性质，并给出五输出与三维 Hamming 立方体上的全支撑反例；所有最优性声明均附有精确的原-对偶证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fisher 一致性是结构化预测中替代损失（surrogate loss）能否在极限意义下恢复贝叶斯最优解码器（argmax）的关键保证。此前已知"任务损失为度量且每个输出三元组有公共测地点"是 SSVM 一致性的必要条件，但该条件是否充分、以及它与坐标式 argmax 解码器的实际兼容性尚未澄清；作者旨在填补这一理论空白并刻画反例的最小规模。

Method: 作者借助多面体/线性规划框架，以原-对偶对偶性构造精确的最优性证书；通过对度量空间（单位星、树度量、K_{m,n} 完全二部图族、Hamming 立方体）上的得分向量与损失几何进行系统分类与极小构造，给出边界分布和全支撑分布两类反例，并给出 argmax 一致性的充要刻画。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 公共测地点条件不足以保证坐标式 argmax 解码器的一致性，四输出单位星即给出最小反例；(2) 正加权树度量上 argmax 一致性当且仅当树为路径，分支树仅在边界分布失败；(3) 满足公共测地点条件的度量中，五输出是构造全支撑反例的最小规模，K_{2,3} 是最小的 K_{m,n} 族成员；(4) 三维 Hamming 立方体也存在全支撑反例；所有最优性结果均有精确的原-对偶证书。

Conclusion: 这些反例揭示了具体的"解码器缺口"：在多面体设定下，存在一个保证校准链接（calibrated link）存在的嵌入，并不意味着指定的 argmax 链接在每个替代风险最小化器上都得到验证；因此必须将嵌入存在性与解码器验证区分开来，对解码器一致性提出更强的验证要求。

Abstract: A known necessary condition for Fisher consistency of the structured support vector machine requires the task loss to be a metric for which every output triple has a common geodesic point. We show that this condition is not sufficient for the canonical coordinate-wise argmax decoder. A four-output unit star admits an exactly optimal score vector whose maximizers are all strictly non-Bayes, and four outputs are minimal among metrics satisfying the condition. We then completely classify positively weighted tree metrics whose vertex set is the output space: argmax consistency holds if and only if the tree is a path. The failure on branching trees is confined to boundary distributions; every tree retains the argmax property at every full-support distribution. Among metrics satisfying the common-geodesic condition, five outputs are necessary and sufficient for a full-support counterexample; $K_{2,3}$ is the smallest member of an infinite $K_{m,n}$ family. We additionally give a full-support counterexample for the three-dimensional Hamming cube. All optimality claims have exact primal-dual certificates. The counterexamples expose a concrete decoder gap: in this polyhedral setting, an embedding can guarantee the existence of a calibrated link without validating a prescribed argmax link on every surrogate-risk minimizer.

</details>


### [379] [Profit based evaluation of machine learning for nitrogen recommendations in winter wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27205)
*Xulong Wang,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以利润而非预测准确率来评估氮肥施用量建议。在892条实测产量响应曲线上构建测试平台，并遍历氮肥/谷物价格比。结果表明机器学习在预测上失效，价格正常时其利润也不如标准建议，但将机器学习输出作为标准建议的"校正项"（配合阻尼）可削减约1/4至43%的利润损失，且无需重训即可跨地块推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冬小麦氮肥用量在季节前就需确定，而当时价格与天气未知。英国标准施肥建议不随价格变化，但近年价格波动会使最优施氮量移动数十千克/公顷。机器学习常被提出作为解决方案，但通常只按预测精度评价——预测准并不等于推荐量更赚钱，因此需要直接按利润来评判施肥建议。

Method: 核心思路是直接以"放弃的利润"（profit forgone）在实测产量响应曲线上给氮肥建议打分。基于两个长期英国试验站的892条响应曲线构建测试基准，扫描氮肥对谷物价格比以覆盖所有价格情景，从而对比多种机器学习模型与标准建议。

Result: 在该基准上机器学习作为预测器失败：没有模型能在农场可容忍误差内恢复最优施氮量，且基准噪声表明任何模型都做不到；正常价格下所有机器学习模型的利润都不如标准建议。收益在别处：在模型之后施加一个简单的校正步骤，可将利润损失削减约1/4；该固定校正无需重训即在第二站点削减43%损失。更好的模型和额外特征无增益。标准建议加阻尼校正的混合方案可去除偏差并削减罕见的大额损失。同一价格扫描还量化了减排成本，其与当前碳价格相当。

Conclusion: 机器学习的价值在于作为标准建议的"利润评分校正项"，而非作为其替代品。混合方案（标准建议+阻尼校正）是更稳健的施肥决策方式，同时该方法还能用价格扫描为排放削减定价。

Abstract: Nitrogen rates for winter wheat are set before the season, under unknown prices and weather. The standard UK advice does not respond to prices, yet recent price swings moved the most profitable rate by tens of kilograms per hectare. Machine learning is often proposed as the fix. However, it is usually judged on prediction accuracy, and accurate prediction does not by itself make the recommended rate more profitable. Our insight is to score nitrogen advice directly by the profit it forgoes on measured yield response curves. We build a test bench on 892 such curves from two long running UK experiments, and sweep the nitrogen to grain price ratio to cover all price scenarios. On this bench, machine learning fails as a predictor. No model recovers the best rate within farm tolerance, and the benchmark noise shows none can. At normal prices, every model also loses to the standard advice on profit. The gain sits elsewhere. A simple correction step applied after the model cuts profit losses by a quarter, while better models and extra features give no gain. The same frozen correction cuts losses by 43% at the second site without any retraining. A hybrid of standard advice plus a damped correction removes bias and trims rare large losses. The same price sweep also prices emission cuts, at a cost comparable to current carbon prices. Machine learning therefore pays as a profit scored correction to standard advice, not as its replacement.

</details>


### [380] [HALO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Language-Aligned IMU Foundation Model for Open-Set Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27233)
*Zihan Ding,Liyu Zhang,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HALO是一个面向IMU的领域专用基础模型，通过两阶段训练（异构感知自监督预训练+同义词感知软对比学习的文本对齐），解决传感异构性与开放集泛化两大挑战，以~35M参数在8项聚合指标上全面超越五个SOTA基线，零样本开放集准确率提升13.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAR领域缺乏能跨主体、设备、活动泛化的统一模型，主要面临两个关键挑战：一是传感异构性（采样率、通道配置、传感器位置差异），二是对未见活动与标签词汇的开放集泛化能力差。

Method: 两阶段训练框架：阶段1用异构感知自监督学习预训练IMU编码器，包括自适应池化分词、通道无关特征提取、以及将自然语言传感描述注入各通道嵌入的上下文传感条件化；阶段2通过同义词感知的软对比学习将IMU编码器与文本嵌入对齐，实现基于余弦相似度检索的开放集识别，无需逐数据集分类器。

Result: 在10个公共HAR数据集上训练、7个留出数据集上评估，HALO在全部8项聚合指标上优于五个SOTA基线；在基线匹配输入下仍在4项设置中的3项领先。仅用约35M可训练参数（比MOMENT少10倍），将87个训练标签上的零样本开放集准确率提升13.7个百分点。在两个严重分布漂移数据集上所有模型包括HALO均零样本崩溃。

Conclusion: HALO证明了面向IMU的异构感知与语言对齐基础模型能有效提升跨域开放集HAR的泛化能力，且参数量高效；同时揭示了极端分布漂移仍是开放集HAR的未解难题。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using inertial measurement units (IMUs) enables a wide range of applications, yet the field still lacks a unified model that can generalize across diverse subjects, devices, and activities. Training such a model is difficult due to two key challenges: sensing heterogeneity -- differences in sampling rates, channel configurations, and sensor placements -- and poor generalization to unseen activities and label vocabularies. We introduce HALO (Heterogeneity-Aware Language-aligned Open-set model), a domain-specific IMU foundation model that addresses both challenges through a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 pretrains the IMU encoder with heterogeneity-aware self-supervised learning, including adaptive-pooling tokenization, channel-independent feature extraction, and contextualized sensor conditioning that injects natural-language sensor descriptions into each channel embedding. Stage 2 aligns this IMU encoder with text embeddings via synonym-aware soft contrastive learning, enabling open-set recognition via cosine-similarity retrieval without per-dataset classifiers. Trained on 10 public HAR datasets and evaluated on 7 held-out datasets, HALO outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on all 8 aggregate metrics, and still leads on 3 of 4 settings under baseline-matched inputs. Despite using only ~35M trainable parameters -- 10x fewer than the latest foundation model MOMENT (341.2M) -- HALO improves zero-shot open-set accuracy, measured over all 87 training labels, by 13.7 percentage points. On two further datasets with severe distribution shift, every model including HALO collapses zero-shot. A video demonstration of HALO's performance in real world is available at https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU

</details>


### [381] [Importance Scoring of Transformer Attention Heads in Learning Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27241)
*Ahmad Jad Allah,Kazi F. Akhter,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将重要性评分指标应用于多头Transformer在表格数据学习中的可解释性分析。通过在40个多样化表格数据集上的实验，提出基于注意力头重要性分数的剪枝方法：按最低重要性分数逐步移除注意力头时模型性能下降最小（占72.5%实验样例），而先移除最重要注意力头会导致最大性能下降。分析六个注意力层发现重要头分散在各层，无一致的层内趋势；与图像和语言领域不同，表格数据中注意力头的重要性随数据集模式和特征空间的不同而显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算密集且不透明的深度学习模型可以通过分析其数据转换方式来更好地理解和优化。尽管深度Transformer已在计算机视觉和自然语言处理中被广泛研究，但其在表格数据上的应用仍相对未充分探索。本文旨在弥补这一空白，为表格数据领域的Transformer模型提供可解释性分析工具。

Method: 提出一种重要性评分度量（head importance score）来量化多头Transformer中每个注意力头的重要性。在40个不同的表格数据集上开展实验，系统性地渐进移除注意力头（分别从最低分和最高分头开始），观察模型分类性能的下降情况，并分析不同注意力层间的头部重要性分布及其跨数据集的可变性与一致性。

Result: 在72.5%的实验样例中，逐步移除最低重要性分数的头时模型对性能下降的鲁棒性最强；反之，先移除最重要头会带来最大性能下降。六个注意力层中重要头分散分布，无一致的层内分布趋势。不同于图像和语言领域，表格数据集中注意力头的重要性随数据集模式（schema）和特征空间的不同而显著变化。

Conclusion: 所提出的重要性评分能够改善Transformer架构的效率和冗余度，可指导注意力头的剪枝与网络优化。作者公开了用于测量注意力头重要性的源代码，以促进该领域后续研究与复现。

Abstract: Computationally demanding and opaque deep learning models can be better understood and optimized by analyzing how they transform data. While deep transformers have been widely studied in computer vision and natural language processing, their application in tabular data remains relatively underexplored. This paper presents one of the first applications of an importance-scoring metric to interpret multi-head transformer models in learning from tabular data. Experiments conducted on 40 diverse tabular datasets demonstrate robustness to head drops based on the proposed head importance score. In 72.5\% of experimental examples, the model remains most resilient to performance drops when heads with the lowest importance scores are gradually removed. In contrast, removing the most important attention head first results in the greatest reduction in classification performance. A closer look at individual head importance scores across six attention layers reveals that important heads are scattered across layers, with no consistent layer-specific trends. In contrast to the image and language domains, the importance of individual attention heads varies considerably across tabular datasets with different schemas and feature spaces. The proposed importance score can improve efficiency and redundancy within transformer architectures. We make the source code for measuring the importance of individual attention heads publicly available.

</details>


### [382] [Circuit Condensation: Post-Training that Concentrates a Behavior's Causal Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27254)
*Sai Adith Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出电路压缩（Circuit Condensation）方法，通过逐轮剪枝低归因边并训练低秩适配器以最小化因果图，将行为浓缩到更小的可解释电路中。在4种行为、8个模型上，30/32设置中压缩电路小于最强的冻结基线，平均缩小8.1倍，最高316倍；并证明权重更新而非单纯搜索驱动了缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结（frozen）电路发现常返回数百条边，难以检查、比较或穷举验证，因此需要一种将行为浓缩进更小因果图的方法，以便获得可验证、可比较的电路。

Method: 每轮先剪除低归因边，再训练低秩适配器（low-rank adapter）使其通过保留下来的边匹配原模型；仅当任务性能和通用能力保持时保留该次剪枝。此外通过重复搜索但不更新权重的对照实验分离搜索与权重更新的贡献。

Result: 在30/32设置中压缩电路小于最强冻结基线，平均小8.1倍、最大316倍；29/32设置中无权重更新的重复搜索产生更大电路。对19个电路全子集测试发现11个不可再缩减，其余含有可移除边；成对消融揭示边间依赖。间接宾语识别中，压缩隔离出24个头（17个有记录角色），匹配冻结电路有61个头（36个无记录），即形成已发表机制的充分子电路。

Conclusion: 电路压缩能以远小于冻结基线的方式产出充分且可验证的子电路，追踪原模型的next-token分布并预测其错误；同时印证电路边之间存在相互依赖，不能孤立理解。

Abstract: One approach to mechanistic interpretability explains behavior through circuits: the components and connections that carry it. Frozen discovery often returns hundreds of edges, making them hard to inspect, compare, or verify exhaustively. We introduce Circuit Condensation, which post-trains models to concentrate behaviors into smaller causal graphs. Each round prunes low-attribution edges and trains a low-rank adapter to match the original through what remains, retaining the cut only if task performance and general capability survive. Across four behaviors and eight models, condensed circuits are smaller than the strongest frozen baseline in 30 of 32 settings, by $8.1\times$ on average and up to $316\times$. Repeating the search without weight updates produces larger circuits in 29 of 32 settings, showing that weight updates, rather than search alone, drive the reduction. Testing every subset of 19 circuits finds 11 that cannot be reduced and reveals removable edges in the rest. Pair ablations expose dependencies between edges, showing that their effects cannot be understood independently. On indirect object identification, condensation isolates 24 heads, 17 of them with documented roles, against 61 heads and 36 undocumented ones for the matched frozen circuit: a sufficient sub-circuit of the published mechanism rather than a reconstruction of it. The resulting circuit tracks the original model's next-token distribution and predicts its errors.

</details>


### [383] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜空间演化算子网络（LEON），将世界动作模型（WAM）中的潜空间状态转移实现方式从常见的 Transformer 式 token 交互架构，改为基于受控 Koopman 生成器视角的算子传播与加性强迫机制，从而在潜空间 WAM 中建立演化特有的归纳偏置，提升闭环控制性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间 WAM 通过在隐表示空间中预测场景状态演化来增强机器人策略，避免完整外观级生成。然而，现有潜空间转移普遍采用 Transformer 预测器，其归纳结构以 token 间交互为核心，而非面向时间演化过程，导致架构选择与任务本质不匹配。作者主张将“转移实现方式”作为独立于预测表示与策略耦合方式的重要架构维度加以研究。

Method: 作者引入 Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON)：在一个可学习的可观测空间中，通过上下文调制的算子传播（context-modulated operator-based propagation）加上加性强迫（additive forcing）建立转移模型。其理论基础是受控 Koopman 生成器视角，即把与上下文相关的转移变化组织在一个共享的演化算子结构上，同时保留互补的加性变化路径。作者在受控动力系统上验证该演化特定归纳偏置及算子传播与强迫的互补作用，并在两种不同的 WAM 集成方式下进行测试（包括完全替换转移模块）。

Result: 在受控动力系统上验证了演化特定归纳偏置以及算子传播与加性强迫的互补角色；在两种将潜空间预测以不同方式集成进策略的 WAM 公式中，LEON 均提升了闭环性能和鲁棒性，并且在完全替换转移模块的情况下依然有效。

Conclusion: 研究表明，转移实现方式（transition realization）是潜空间 WAM 中具有决定意义的架构选择：与纯 token 交互式 Transformer 转移相比，面向时间演化的算子式结构能更好地支持机器人闭环控制，为潜空间 WAM 的架构设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [384] [MM-Spectrum: Multimodal Multi-spectral Molecular Structural Elucidation with a Stable MoE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27286)
*Hai-tao Yu,Nan Min,Zheng Fang,Hongyu Zhan,Yusen Tan,Yuhan Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-Spectrum，一种面向多模态多光谱数据、用于分子结构解析的稀疏专家混合（Mixture-of-Experts）框架。针对多光谱信号高度异质导致的多模态失衡问题，MM-Spectrum引入显式的模态感知路由机制（在路由中暴露光谱身份），并采用共享专家和交互专家及异质专家容量设计，以提取模态特有信息与跨模态协同信息、抑制噪声干扰。在完整模态、双模态和缺失模态设定下均取得一致且显著的性能提升，并通过消融实验和可解释性分析加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
